# LOOM 2226 — CANON I: WORLD, HISTORY & FRONTIER
## Corrected Consolidated Baseline v2.4

**Status:** GOVERNING CANON  
**Freeze:** 22 August 2026 — mobility/accessibility, metric-architecture and runtime consolidation  
**Institutional amendment:** 24 August 2026 — law/security/merchant-reserve/causal-societal integration; no propulsion or census change


**v2.4 promotion record:** Preserves the complete v2.3 world-history/xenoarchaeology amendment and adds the post-review Solar mobility/accessibility overlay, traffic/shipyard narrative classes, and explicit model-status discipline. Exact fleet counts, fares, OD demand and metric terminal numbers are not promoted.

**Inherited v2.3 record:** Major world-history amendment. The previous “no verified alien technology” campaign-start baseline is explicitly superseded. 1I/ʻOumuamua, the Reef and Kite lineages, Terminal Mobilization Pattern, Artifact Economy, Continuity Naturalism, Expansionism and The Convergence are promoted while the Great Filter, Builder motive and Builder source remain unresolved. No governing propulsion constant changes.

This volume consolidates the setting-facing canon: foundational world rules, Solar geography,
population/economy, institutions, and the publicly known extrasolar frontier.

**Authority:** Where duplicated material differs, this v2.4 consolidation and the explicit v2.4 manifest govern. Technical propulsion authority remains CANON II; hidden truth/GM authority remains CANON III.


## v2.4 LLM runtime routing

This is a consolidated source volume. Embedded source headers may retain older version labels for provenance; **the outer v2.4 authority controls whenever they conflict**.

For ordinary game/world questions:

```text
world/history/public frontier      -> CANON I v2.4
Solar place / narrative geography -> Atlas v3.2 section embedded below
engineering/flight                -> CANON II v2.4
truth layers / hidden graph       -> CANON III v2.4
checks / HUD / runtime procedure  -> Core Mechanics v0.5
```

### Mobility rules promoted in v2.4

1. Mature Solar geography is still overwhelmingly torch-era; no demographic retcon is permitted merely because metric is fast.
2. **Torch moves mass; Metric moves time/urgency; Loom moves the frontier and interstellar packet state.** Inside Sol, laser/radio at `c` outruns every certified metric ship; metric moves people and physical custody, not faster-than-light information.
3. Metric commercial capacity is constrained by scarce qualified relational capital and certification, but exact fleet size remains a model output.
4. Major gateways are legitimate traffic/infrastructure objects, but a roadstead, port, traffic-control boundary and physical metric-certification volume are different objects.
5. The Atlas uses qualitative traffic/yard classes as canon and retains numerical throughput only as provisional model calibration.
6. A full Solar individual-ship metric traffic simulation is not the default. Use coarse capacity/queue modeling only where it changes events or economics.
7. All exact accessibility/fare/fleet outputs from `ACCESS-COMM v0.1` remain **PROVISIONAL MODEL**, pending hostile review and better OD/fleet calibration.

### Model-status rule

A number may be presented as setting fact only if its owning source marks it LOCKED/DERIVED canon. For research-model outputs, expose the model ID and status. Do not average contradictory or differently scoped provisional models into a fake consensus.

# SOURCE DIVISION — Foundational Canon

**Consolidated from:** `LOOM_2226_Foundational_Canon_v1.6.md`



**LOOM**

2226: Foundational Setting, Physics & Technology Canon

Version 1.6 • Xenoarchaeology / Terminal Mobilization Promotion Freeze: 21 August 2026

**Baseline status:** **LOCKED CANON — LOOM 2226 Canon Baseline v2.3**

| **PURPOSE** This document supersedes Foundational Canon v1.5 as the governing foundational world baseline. It preserves the August 2226 flight-test and causal-forensics architecture while explicitly promoting the 1I/ʻOumuamua archaeological history, three verified technological lineages, Builder First/Second Erasure framework, Terminal Mobilization Pattern, Artifact Economy, Continuity Naturalism, Expansionism and The Convergence. Physics remains governed by M1/M2 and the certified propulsion stack; alien archaeology does not create a third miracle or a hidden Great Filter answer. |
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

**Core design sentence**

> *Humanity has built miracles, brought all of its old bullshit with it, and has just opened the first real door to the stars.*

**Campaign horizon**

> *The first five minutes of the interstellar age.*

# 1. Document Control & Canon Rules

This is a design-bible document, not an in-universe encyclopedia. It records the setting’s intended simulation rules, the beliefs of people living in 2226, and the mysteries that even the designers deliberately refuse to settle. Those are different epistemic categories and must stay different.

## 1.1 Canon hierarchy

- Locked canon outranks working or provisional canon. Later explicit revisions outrank this document.

- Open questions stay open. A future GM, assistant, or collaborator must preserve ambiguity rather than silently filling gaps.

- GM-only deep models exist to keep the simulation coherent; they are not automatically true in-universe theories and are not player knowledge.

- Hidden cosmological possibilities are not to be leaked simply because the design document acknowledges them.

- Generated concept-art microtext, exact masses, capacities, class names, operator names, and labels are illustrative unless repeated in the textual canon.

- The simulation never changes objective reality to reward genre recognition. Mundane, fraudulent, human, technological, biological, astronomical, and genuinely anomalous explanations can coexist.

## 1.2 Canon-status vocabulary

| **Status**                   | **Meaning**                                                                                                                           |
|------------------------------|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| LOCKED CANON                 | Governing rule for setting design and play until explicitly revised.                                                                  |
| PROVISIONAL CANON            | Adopted working history or implementation detail that may be refined without changing the setting’s identity.                         |
| CAMPAIGN TRUTH               | Objective truth established inside one campaign continuity. It governs that campaign but does not automatically rewrite setting-wide canon or other campaigns. |
| GM-ONLY DEEP MODEL           | A consistency scaffold available to the simulation. It is not necessarily known by 2226 science and may not be the ultimate ontology. |
| INTENTIONALLY UNRESOLVED     | The setting gains power by not deciding this yet. Do not “solve” it merely to complete a document.                                    |
| ILLUSTRATIVE / WORKING VALUE | A number, name, or example used for engineering feel or playtest; it can move when the detailed model is built.                       |

## 1.3 Simulation truth vs. human belief

The engine distinguishes at least four layers: objective simulated state; what instruments and records can physically establish; what institutions and individuals believe; and what the player has actually learned. A correct theory does not become player knowledge merely because the GM knows it. An incorrect in-world theory remains real as culture, policy, and motivation.

A campaign may resolve a question that remains intentionally unresolved in the setting bible. Once play causally establishes that answer, it becomes **Campaign Truth** for that continuity. It does not silently become universal canon. Conversely, an intentionally unresolved setting question does not prevent a campaign from discovering a concrete answer when doing so creates a stronger causal world.

| **GM RULE** Simulation knows reality. The player knows only what could have reached them through sensors, testimony, records, inference, and physically carried information. Campaign discoveries stay inside their campaign unless explicitly promoted into setting-wide canon. |
|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

## 1.4 What this setting is trying to feel like

The baseline experience is grounded, tactile, technically legible spacefaring: fuel, heat, reaction mass, maintenance, orbital geography, sensor uncertainty, money, crew competence, latency, and institutional friction. Cosmic mystery only works because the ordinary universe is coherent enough to trust first.

- Elite Dangerous-scale awe and navigational freedom, but with stronger causal engineering and a smaller, more intimate ship.

- The Expanse for within-system logistics, acceleration, danger, crew scale, and politics.

- Dune / Event Horizon conceptual influence for FTL: not “go faster through normal space,” but a translation event through deeper relational structure.

- Solaris / Arrival influence for contact and cognition: the alien, if encountered, need not be a humanoid adversary or a puzzle designed for humans.

- Cosmic horror as ontological threat, not a parade of monsters or “Ancients did everything.”

- A simulation in which physics generates engineering; engineering generates economics; economics generates institutions; institutions generate human stories.

## 1.6 v1.6 promotion summary

Version 1.6 preserves the full physical, institutional, personhood and Strangeness architecture of the prior baseline while making one major world-history amendment: humanity has known since 2066 that extraterrestrial technological intelligence existed. The revision promotes the 1I/ʻOumuamua history, Builder biology and Nursery, deliberate First Erasure, restricted Second Erasure/TID evidence, Reef and Kite archaeology, Terminal Mobilization Pattern, Artifact Economy, Continuity Naturalism, Expansionism and The Convergence. It explicitly preserves the two-miracle budget, human engineering ownership of Mc-299m/NRE, survivorship/selection uncertainty, and the firewall between alien archaeology and High Strangeness. The Great Filter remains an open scientific problem rather than hidden setting truth.

# 2. Campaign Baseline: 2226 and the First Interstellar Age

| **LOCKED CANON** The campaign begins in 2226, only a few years after the first successful crewed interstellar translation. Most adults remember when another star was physically unreachable. |
|-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

## 2.1 Technological chronology

| **Period**    | **Development** |
|---------------|-----------------|
| 2017          | 1I/ʻOumuamua is discovered. Its interstellar trajectory and nongravitational acceleration become enduring scientific anomalies; neither proves artificiality at the time. |
| 2031          | **First Look** launches on a high-energy chemical/gravity/Oberth architecture to overtake 1I. |
| 2060–2100     | Permanent orbital and Lunar industry expands; durable Mars settlement becomes a serious civilizational project. |
| 2066          | First Look performs a high-speed remote encounter at hundreds of AU. Imaging, lidar and tomography establish manufactured internal geometry. Extraterrestrial technological intelligence becomes verified science. |
| 2074          | **Second Chase** launches for rendezvous using heavy launch/gravity assist/Oberth plus fission-electric cruise and terminal braking. |
| 2100–2140     | Practical high-performance fusion propulsion transforms Solar-System travel. Torch ships emerge. |
| 2125          | Second Chase rendezvous with 1I. Human physical access begins; the later-named **Access Cascade** begins or is triggered in close temporal association with entry. |
| 2130s–2140s   | Alien superheavy-parent forensic evidence redirects a major human nuclear/materials research race toward the A≈299, Z≈115 neighborhood. |
| 2140–2190     | Torch technology becomes mundane infrastructure. Mars, Belt and outer-system industry expand into a mature spacefaring civilization. |
| 2150s         | Human laboratories synthesize Mc-299 in relevant experimental quantities; the result is initially disappointing. |
| 2160s         | Human researchers identify the useful metastable Mc-299m state; host-lattice / NRE architecture becomes the central engineering problem. |
| late 2170s–2180s | Tiny reproducible excitation-correlated relational residuals become controlled subluminal metric effects. |
| 2190–2215     | Metric drives progress from laboratories to government and corporate vessels. They remain expensive, rare and strategic. |
| c. 2215–2220  | A deeper-topology breakthrough turns nonlocal translation from theory into experiment. |
| c. 2221       | First successful unmanned interstellar translation. |
| c. 2223       | First successful crewed interstellar translation. |
| 2224–2225     | First Tau Ceti crewed surveys identify and then verify the independent **Reef** technological lineage. |
| 2226          | First crewed Epsilon Eridani campaign verifies the independent **Kite** technological lineage. Campaign begins with three known technological lineages, no living alien civilization, and a still-tiny interstellar frontier. |

## 2.2 Frontier scale

- There are not hundreds of settled star systems. Permanent extrasolar settlements are few, embryonic, or not yet truly permanent.

- Nearby systems may contain probes, survey beacons, temporary camps, government/corporate footholds, or isolated experiments. Many nearby stars remain genuinely unexplored by humans.

- A Loom-capable vessel is closer to an ocean-going ship at the start of an Age of Exploration than to an ordinary family car.

- Only a few dozen Loom-capable systems may exist, with fewer genuinely reliable crew-rated vessels. Exact registry counts remain open.

- The frontier outruns law, resupply, supervision, synchronization, and rescue. Freedom and danger arise from the same attenuation of institutional power.

## 2.3 The first-five-minutes effect

Loom travel did not grow up alongside an interstellar society. It arrived into an old Solar-System civilization with laws, banks, militaries, religions, corporations, universities, unions, intelligence services, insurers, organized crime, and two centuries of technological memory. The first interstellar disputes are therefore not “new civilization” problems. They are old human institutions discovering that across Loom links jurisdiction, supervision and synchronized legal state can now be physically outrun. Inside Sol, this does **not** mean a metric ship outruns a warrant broadcast by laser or radio; electromagnetic information still propagates faster than every certified metric vessel.

Adults remember the first probe returning from another star. Careers, marriages, pensions, national identities, scientific reputations, and political programs were formed before the frontier existed. This creates a historically unusual society in which the impossible became routine within one generation but has not yet become culturally boring.

# 3. Human Civilization in the Solar System

Humanity became a spacefaring civilization before it became an interstellar one. Loom travel therefore arrives into a populated, industrialized, politically contested Solar System rather than creating space civilization from scratch.

## 3.1 Earth

Earth remains the center of human population, culture, finance, and political legitimacy. It is materially rich, densely populated, highly networked, and heavily administered.

- Clean energy, advanced medicine, automation, and environmental restoration have improved daily life enormously compared with 2026.

- High population and complex infrastructure produce layers of licensing, identity, zoning, risk controls, automated compliance, and institutional dependency.

- Earth is probably the safest and most comfortable human environment ever built; to some people it is also intolerably managed.

- Long life and institutional memory have entrenched elite networks and slowed generational turnover.

- Earth is not a single neat “United Earth.” Strong states, federations, blocs, cities, transnational institutions, and planetary bodies overlap.

## 3.2 Mars

Mars is a substantial engineered civilization with its own industry, research base, political institutions, and cultural confidence. It is not terraformed; human life still depends on systems that can kill when neglected.

- Martian culture prizes competence, maintenance, and systems thinking.

- A genuine civilizational nationalism has emerged around a simple claim: “We built this place.”

- Mars sees extrasolar expansion as both opportunity and strategic threat.

- Martian governments tend to be more comfortable than many Earth jurisdictions with technocracy, occupational adaptation, and emergency authority.

- Earth can call Mars authoritarian; Mars can call Earth structurally unserious. Both can produce evidence.

## 3.3 The Belt and outer system

The Belt is a mining, industrial, service, scientific, and transport frontier rather than one unified polity. “Belter” is a broad cultural label; political identity is usually more specific: which station, habitat, charter, union, corporation, municipality, family network, or route?

- Some settlements are democratic municipalities; some are cooperatives; some are corporate facilities; some are effective microstates; some are criminal.

- Company-town dynamics are common wherever one operator controls oxygen, docking, housing, identity, and payroll. Control of heat, water, pressure integrity, environmental services or docking can become a uniquely coercive form of power, but it does not create a blanket legal right to endanger residents; public law, cooperative ownership, labor protections and emergency doctrine vary sharply by jurisdiction.

- Outer-system culture rewards improvisation, repair skill, resourcefulness, and distrust of distant authority.

- Long communication delays already gave Solar-System societies experience with asynchronous governance before the Loom; interstellar delay pushes the problem much further.

## 3.4 Layered identity and strategic rivalry

Planetary identities layer on top of older national, linguistic, religious, class, professional, and local identities. Mars has strong nationalism. The Belt has broad cultural solidarities but no single state. Earth remains politically plural. Earth–Mars strategic rivalry is a durable background condition rather than a simple Cold War clone.

Governments and corporations respond to Loom technology with export controls, classification, patents, licensing, insurance restrictions, military planning, claim law, and infrastructure investment. The frontier, however, moves faster than any of those systems can be standardized.

| **LOCKED CANON** Governments and corporations are complicated institutions, not default villains. Humanity is competent, beautiful, idealistic, exploitative, bureaucratic, generous, violent, and contradictory enough that the civilization being built is worth protecting. |
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

### 3.5 Power is layered

| **DERIVED CANON CANDIDATE** Regional centrality does not equal sovereignty. Population, economic output, industrial throughput, infrastructure control, certification authority, political legitimacy, intelligence and military/security power are different maps. |
|-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

Civil jurisdiction is local unless an actual constitutional history establishes otherwise. Transit, rescue, standards, metrology, survey and security institutions can span systems because their physical networks span systems. A gateway polity can be influential without governing every neighboring moon or station.

**Institutional morphology is not standardized.** Shared physical problems can produce similar functions without producing identical organizations. Dense cislunar civilization supports specialized traffic, standards and strategic bodies; Mars uses a coordinating compact above strong regional governments; Jupiter integrates radiation-aware navigation and rescue; Saturn relies more heavily on intermoon treaty institutions; Uranus bundles several network functions into its free-space exchange; Neptune uses a Triton–habitat compact; Kuiper and Oort rely on sparse route, survey and metrology networks. Repeated function is not evidence of one constitutional template.

Mars is the second broad advanced-industrial civilizational pole of the inner Solar System. Cislunar space is the largest off-Earth concentration of high-end fabrication, finance and proof infrastructure, but neither is a sovereign monopoly.

## 3.6 Jurisdiction, law enforcement, and causal enforcement

There is no single Solar-System police service with plenary criminal authority. Criminal-intelligence exchanges, warrant-clearing systems, biometric/synthetic identity hashes and treaty notices are federated across sovereign and local institutions. Inside Sol those notices propagate by ordinary light-speed communications; **a subluminal metric ship cannot normally outrun the information itself**. What remains contestable is whether the receiving jurisdiction recognizes the warrant, trusts the provenance, has treaty authority, can identify the person or vessel, and is willing and able to enforce.

Operational authority is layered:

- **Registry / charter authority:** in deep heliocentric space, vessel registry, charter and flag law remain important default legal anchors.
- **Local / port authority:** once a ship enters a governed roadstead, port, habitat or gateway, local safety, customs and criminal law can become decisive.
- **Treaty / transjurisdictional offenses:** many jurisdictions claim or recognize unusually broad authority for mass-casualty habitat sabotage, uncontrolled relativistic-kinetic attacks, prohibited metric/Loom interference and severe continuity/personhood crimes. The precise offense and jurisdictional hook are not universal.
- **Standards / certification power:** standards bodies can revoke trusted coefficient sets, certification recognition or infrastructure access. That can make lawful high-trust operation impossible without physically switching the drive off.
- **Insurer / counterparty power:** an underwriter, lender or major port can render a hull commercially radioactive by refusing coverage, finance or access. This is economic leverage, not sovereignty.

Metric policing therefore concentrates on **endpoints, access and mathematics**: acquisition/collapse volumes, roadsteads, traffic control, metrology, yards, repair/refuel, certification and identity/provenance checks. Mid-course interception of a fast metric vessel is possible only when geometry and an explicit operational model support it; it is not the default police chase.

Across Loom links the legal problem changes fundamentally. No independent FTL channel exists, so warrants, court orders, market state, government authority and forensic updates physically ride the packet network. Interstellar law can therefore be authentic and still be causally old.


# 4. Life, Xenoarchaeology & Cosmic-Horror Doctrine

## 4.1 Scientific worldview at campaign start

| **LOCKED CANON** By 2226 humanity has verified exactly **three independent extraterrestrial technological lineages**: the 1I Builders, the Reef lineage and the Kite lineage. No living extraterrestrial technological individual, extant technological civilization, or verified communicative alien intelligence has been encountered. |
|---|

The existence of technological extraterrestrial intelligence is settled science. The unresolved question is why every verified case is archaeological.

The 1I Builders were confirmed artificial in 2066 and physically accessed in 2125. Their Nursery, deliberate biological First Erasure, artifact loss during the Access Cascade and broad contribution to the human Mc research path are public historical facts. Reef archaeology was verified at Tau Ceti in 2225; Kite archaeology is being verified at Epsilon Eridani during 2226.

The Tau Ceti and Epsilon Eridani sites are **not canonically the species' home systems**. They are terminal-era archaeological complexes whose relationship to original homeworlds remains uncertain.

## 4.2 Evidence-state model for alien archaeology and first living contact

First contact is no longer synonymous with first evidence of alien technology. Humanity has abundant archaeological evidence and zero living interlocutors. The GM tracks distinct evidence states rather than treating archaeology, life and communication as one ladder.

| **Evidence state** | **Examples** | **Default interpretation discipline** |
|---|---|---|
| Biological | biosignature, cell, fossil, complex organism, developmental residue | Require contamination control, provenance, independent replication and evolutionary plausibility. |
| Technological | manufactured geometry, engineered isotope ratios, artifact, infrastructure | Distinguish technology from geology, human contamination, automated systems and later alteration. |
| Cognitive / communicative | adaptive signaling, language-like structure, apparent agency, surviving state | Do not equate pattern with personhood; establish current agency rather than assuming a dead system is a mind. |
| Historical / provenance | ruins, custody records, repeated observations, pre-Cascade scans | Age, source, chain of custody and causal availability matter as much as content. |
| Terminal-behavioral | dismantling, continuity infrastructure, launch patterns, identity deletion | Describe observed behavior before inferring motive or common cause. |
| Ontological anomaly | measurements resisting models of locality, identity or causality | Preserve the distinction between model failure and supernatural explanation. Alien provenance does not bypass this rule. |

Unique evidence is also a security object. Raw observations, calibration/provenance data, physical custody, derived models, interpretation and disclosure can belong to different institutions.

## 4.3 The three verified lineages

### 4.3.1 1I Builders

The Builders were macroscopic carbon/water cellular organisms whose mature person architecture required **obligate multi-lineage biological symbiosis plus manufactured developmental cognitive substrate**. Humanity knows their physiology much better than their anatomy. Exact body morphology, language, self-name, social institutions and home system remain unknown.

The public Nursery contained roughly 4,500–5,000 developmental positions. Its hereditary systems were deliberately rendered unreconstructible before the vessel's final propulsion catastrophe. Restricted evidence further establishes deliberate sanitization of individualized synthetic developmental state; the strongest Deep Black interpretation is **Terminal Identity Denial**, meaning destruction optimized against reconstructing complete integrated persons rather than against bulk matter.

TID describes the forensic outcome. Its motive is unknown.

### 4.3.2 Reef lineage

Tau Ceti contains an ancient Reef industrial/founder complex, terminal horizon roughly **1.5–3 million years ago**. Reef intelligence appears to have evolved as large distributed colonial biological networks in an aquatic environment. Late fixed living-industrial infrastructure was systematically harvested into compact founder systems and outbound continuity packages. No Builder-style identity erasure is present.

### 4.3.3 Kite lineage

Epsilon Eridani contains a major Kite terminal/dispersal complex, terminal horizon roughly **80,000–250,000 years ago**. Kite persons appear to have evolved as low-density buoyant atmospheric organisms whose mature engineering favored membranes, thin films and extreme area-to-mass ratios. Late in their history that design philosophy reversed toward dense, shielded departure systems launched in many directions. Numerous failed departure vehicles are verified; no living continuation is known.

## 4.4 Terminal Mobilization Pattern and Great Filter discipline

The scientific term is **Terminal Mobilization Pattern (TMP)**: a late archaeological transition in which a technological civilization reallocates a substantial fraction of fixed industrial capacity toward mobile, dispersed or long-duration continuity infrastructure.

All three verified lineages exhibit TMP strongly enough to justify a serious comparative field. None provides a demonstrated common cause.

| **LOCKED CANON** A universal Great Filter is **not established**. Detection and survivorship bias are first-class unresolved confounders. No canon fact states that AI, germline modification, Mc, Loom, synthetic personhood, TID or any one technology causes TMP. |
|---|

Major live explanations include common external danger, endogenous maturity crisis, rational expansion, archaeological selection bias, panic failure, technological phase change and no common cause at all.

The popular slogan **“They all ran”** is culturally powerful and scientifically stronger than the evidence.

## 4.5 Builder artifact economy and finite evidence

Human access to 1I coincided with or initiated the **Access Cascade**, an irreversible loss of high-information material architecture. By 2226 the original coherent spacecraft is gone. Roughly **6–7 tonnes** of scientifically useful original material survive in authenticated human custody; the exact working ledger remains provisional at 6,572 kg.

The resulting Artifact Order is built around finite samples, provenance, compartmented custody, proprietary analytical capability and intense state/corporate competition. The **Oumuamua Custody Partition** separates physical custody, raw data, analytical control, provenance authority and commercial exploitation rights so that no one institution holds all keys by default.

Alien archaeology generally **collapses search space rather than handing over a working technology**. This rule is especially important for Mc-299m.

## 4.6 Cosmic-horror doctrine

| **Mode** | **Doctrine** |
|---|---|
| Archaeological first contact | Humanity knows alien civilizations through things, residues and absences rather than living ambassadors. |
| Fermi inversion | The disturbing question is no longer “are we alone?” but “why is everybody archaeological?” |
| Truly alien cognition | Intelligence need not map neatly onto human language, motives, individuality or social categories. |
| Evidence faster than explanation | Increase reliable evidence faster than causal closure. More data can make the problem worse. |
| No single cosmology | Do not reduce Builder TID, Reef conversion, Kite dispersal, High Strangeness and Loom physics into one master answer. |
| Human horror | Corporate experiments, illegal reconstruction, politics, atrocity and rational institutional self-protection remain fully capable of being the monster. |
| Observation consequence | Some phenomena may correlate with observation or access, but the Access Cascade is not canonically a self-destruct and alien sites do not become anomalous merely by being alien. |

| **GM RULE** Spectacle may establish reality; it does not establish ontology. Most strange events should still resolve through mundane or conventionally physical causes. The GM never upgrades a compelling pattern into a common cosmic cause without earned evidence. |
|---|

## 4.7 Expertise increases horror

Competence does not make the unknown harmless. It makes violations more legible. The experienced xenoarchaeologist is more disturbed, not less, when three unrelated lineages show terminal continuity mobilization but the statistics remain too weak to identify a cause. The developmental neurobiologist understands exactly what information the Builders destroyed and exactly what is still missing.

Psychological trauma remains psychology and medicine, not a generic Sanity resource.

# 5. Physics Architecture and the Four Propulsion Regimes

| **PHYSICS PRINCIPLE** Whenever possible, keep known physics intact and spend speculative freedom on one clearly identified bridge. The harder the setting leans on real constraints, the more meaningful its few impossible technologies feel. |
|------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

The setting separates local maneuvering, fusion torch propulsion, subluminal metric displacement, and interstellar Loom translation. They are different engineering problems with different resources, operating envelopes, signatures, and failure modes. No single “magic engine” does everything.

## 5.1 Layer 0 — Maneuvering and local thrust

Docking, attitude control, station-keeping, landing assistance where applicable, and close tactical maneuver are handled by real momentum exchange: advanced plasma/electric thrusters, fusion-assisted jets, cold-gas or chemical emergency units, or task-specific mixes.

- Consumes reaction mass. Electrical demand and thermal limits matter.

- Reaction mass is not reactor fuel.

- High-response thrusters remain useful on Loom ships. FTL did not repeal Newton.

## 5.2 Layer 1 — Fusion torch drive

| **LOCKED CANON** Torch drives are mature and common. They underpin the existing Solar-System civilization. Belt haulers, patrol ships, miners, freighters, passenger craft, and private vessels use them. |
|-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

The working reactor lineage is mature direct fusion, provisionally centered on deuterium / helium-3 with a separate reaction-mass stream. Exact fusion chemistry remains adjustable during detailed engineering without changing the setting.

- Fusion fuel supplies energy; commonly water-derived hydrogen or another cheap working fluid supplies reaction mass.

- Water is strategically useful because it can also support life, shielding, manufacturing, and emergency thermal storage.

- Economy mode favors high exhaust velocity and lower mass flow; emergency or combat operation can trade efficiency for thrust.

- Acceleration creates deck gravity. “Down” is toward the engine while the torch pushes.

- A ship that reaches genuinely relativistic velocity experiences ordinary special-relativistic time dilation. Solar-System torch operations usually avoid extreme regimes.

- Heat, nozzle/reactor wear, radiation, maintenance, and reaction mass remain constraints even when fusion energy is abundant.

## 5.3 Layer 2 — Subluminal metric / Alcubierre-derived drive

| **LOCKED CANON** Metric drive is real, subluminal, expensive, and rare. Major governments and large corporations possess it; ordinary private traffic generally does not. It is advanced technology, not mystical technology. |
|-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

The real-science anchors are general-relativistic warp geometries and theoretical work on physically less pathological subluminal configurations. The explicit fictional bridge is that mature metric engineering can use supplied energy and field geometry to generate very large sub-c coordinate displacement without forcing the vessel to acquire the same local kinetic state or crew acceleration a pure rocket would require for an equivalent trip.

- Primary operational cost is energy, thermal load, field hardware stress, and precision rather than reaction mass.

- Typical hardware includes field collars or distributed generators, superconducting buses, pulse-energy banks, curvature sensors, and precision control systems.

- Operational variables include field charge, coherence, thermal load, geometric admissibility, mass balance, core health, navigation clearance, and available power.

- Crew experience little conventional acceleration in metric cruise. Unless rotation is provided, the interior is microgravity.

- The inhabited cavity is engineered for low tidal forces and a lapse near unity, so normal metric cruise does not produce dramatic traveler aging.

- Debris, radiation, field stability, local geometry, and navigation still matter. Very high coordinate displacement becomes progressively expensive and risky.

Current first-generation certified metric performance is vessel- and installation-dependent rather than a universal fleet speed ladder. The inherited courier reference certifies NORMAL 0.268c, FAST 0.437c, EXPEDITE 0.519c, and HARD 0.595c; these values are a reference engineering card, not minimum capabilities for all metric-equipped vessels.

## 5.4 Layer 3 — Loom translation

| **LOCKED CANON** Loom-capable ships are rare as fuck. The technology is prototype/frontier science. There may be only dozens of working drives and fewer genuinely reliable crew-rated systems. |
|-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

Loom travel is not a warp cruise, wormhole passage, hyperspace journey, or locally superluminal particle motion. A bounded region containing the vessel changes its relational embedding relative to ordinary spacetime and resolves into another local spacetime neighborhood.

- Ordinary metric distance remains real. Translation changes relational adjacency, not local speed through the intervening space.

- The system requires enormous stored energy, precise boundary geometry, high-integrity control hardware, and a life-limited translation lattice.

- Translated volume matters. Compact domains are easier and cheaper than large awkward ones, structurally discouraging bulk interstellar freight.

- Navigation difficulty is not linear in light-years. Some distant stars are topologically “nearer” than closer ones.

- Known relationships are characterized empirically; novel relationships are predicted with uncertainty and then physically explored.

- The torch engine remains attached. Loom is not ordinary propulsion.

## 5.5 No FTL communications

| **LOCKED CANON** No information has been demonstrated to propagate through Loom topology independently of a translated physical domain. There is no FTL radio, entanglement telegraph, tachyon channel, or reliable anomalous-memory messaging. |
|-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

- **Across Loom links**, ships become packet carriers for civilization: news, software, model updates, contracts, scientific data, financial state, personal messages, and legal instructions physically ride with vessels. Within Sol, ordinary electromagnetic communications remain faster than metric ships.

- Frontier settlements possess real practical autonomy because Sol cannot supervise them in real time.

- Search and rescue is local. A conventional distress signal from another star is a message to years in the future unless a Loom vessel physically carries it.

- Shipboard AI, medicine, technical libraries, and local compute must work offline. “Call Earth” is not a meaningful frontier contingency plan.

## 5.6 Propulsion-mode comparison

| **Mode** | **Primary physics**                 | **Main cost**                                        | **Crew gravity**                      | **Time behavior**                            | **Typical domain**    |
|----------|-------------------------------------|------------------------------------------------------|---------------------------------------|----------------------------------------------|-----------------------|
| Maneuver | Reaction thrust                     | reaction mass + power                                | as generated by thrust                | ordinary                                     | docking / tactical    |
| Torch    | Fusion-powered reaction propulsion  | fusion fuel + remass + heat                          | acceleration gravity                  | ordinary SR at high v                        | planetary / AU travel |
| Metric   | Engineered sub-c spacetime geometry | energy + heat + coherence                            | microgravity                          | designed near 1:1                            | fast in-system cruise |
| Loom     | Nonlocal relational translation     | stored energy + lattice integrity + solution quality | local cabin state; transition effects | finite local transition, no intervening path | interstellar          |

# 6. The Loom: Theory, Translation Physics & Cosmology

This section deliberately distinguishes current real science, 2226 empirical knowledge, 2226 theoretical interpretation, GM-only consistency models, and questions that remain unresolved even to the setting bible. The equations are effective design notation, not claims that current physics predicts a Loom drive.

## 6.1 Epistemic layers

| **Layer**                | **Meaning**                                                                                                                                                                  |
|--------------------------|------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Real scientific scaffold | Actual 20th/21st-century physics that motivates the fictional question: relativity, quantum information, emergent-spacetime ideas, black-hole information, causal structure. |
| 2226 empirical           | Repeatable observations and engineering constraints measured by humanity.                                                                                                    |
| 2226 theory              | Serious interpretations that organize data but remain revisable.                                                                                                             |
| GM-only deep model       | A private mathematical/conceptual scaffold used to keep the simulation consistent.                                                                                           |
| Intentionally unresolved | Questions the design does not answer: consciousness, agency, ultimate substrate, alien meaning, anomalous-memory ontology.                                                   |

## 6.2 The central speculative move

General relativity makes spacetime geometry dynamical. Modern quantum-gravity research includes serious programs in which spacetime geometry may be emergent, thermodynamic, relational, or connected to patterns of entanglement. None predicts practical FTL. LOOM makes one fictional move: macroscopic metric distance is not the deepest form of physical adjacency, and a sufficiently advanced technology can engineer the relationship between a bounded local domain and that deeper structure.

| **LOCKED CANON** The Loom is not a place, dimension, hyperspace, or tunnel. Ordinary spacetime and Loom connectivity are physically related but non-equivalent. Translation changes relational embedding rather than traversing a hidden corridor. |
|----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

## 6.3 GM-only deep model

| **GM-ONLY DEEP MODEL** The following notation is a consistency scaffold, not an established 2226 ontology. It may be incomplete or even wrong at the deepest level without invalidating the engineering rules that follow. |
|----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

<img src="assets/foundational/media/image4.png" style="width:5.8in;height:0.80105in" />

Deep-model state: relational degrees of freedom, relation structure, causal order, and evolving total state.

For design purposes, U represents a deeper physical state; S its degrees of freedom; R the relational structure among them; ≺ a causal ordering relation; and Ω the evolving total state.

<img src="assets/foundational/media/image5.png" style="width:5.8in;height:0.80105in" />

A bounded projection yields an effective spacetime, metric, fields, and local physical state.

“Projection” does not mean consciousness creates reality. A rock is just as physical as a brain. It means the macroscopic description available inside a local domain may not contain every relational degree of freedom of the deeper state, and the mapping need not be globally invertible.

## 6.4 Loom coherence and non-metric geography

<img src="assets/foundational/media/image6.png" style="width:5.8in;height:0.80105in" />

Illustrative normalized relational coherence between local states A and B.

<img src="assets/foundational/media/image7.png" style="width:5.8in;height:0.80105in" />

Illustrative effective Loom separation. This is fictional effective notation, not a derived quantum-gravity law.

High coherence means two regions are comparatively favorable to relate through the Loom even if they remain separated by many light-years in ordinary metric geometry. Consequently, an 11-light-year relationship can be better than a four-light-year relationship. There are no universal fixed “lanes” or gates; there are broad, narrow, unstable, inaccessible, and drifting solution families.

For design and operations, distinguish **underlying relational coherence** from **operational route burden**. Measured deep coherence between a pair of local states appears approximately reciprocal to current precision. The effective vessel-and-state-specific route burden, conventionally recorded as directional \(\chi_{A\rightarrow B}\), includes origin geometry, destination geometry, domain configuration, lattice capability, energy/thermal margin, uncertainty, and requested arrival-state mapping. Operational \(\chi\) can therefore differ materially by direction without requiring the deeper substrate itself to be fundamentally one-way.

### 6.4.1 Humanity maps the graph by walking it

From Sol, astronomy and Loom metrology can predict candidate relationships, sometimes with very high confidence. They cannot reconstruct the entire network globally. Local measurement at a destination provides much stronger constraints on the relationships extending from that destination.

- Remote theory identifies candidate relationship families.

- Uncrewed probes attempt first crossings as standard doctrine, though physics does not require this.

- A returned probe provides destination-side measurements and closes the empirical loop.

- A directed route may be certified independently. A→B evidence does not establish B→A; a route pair or reliable return plan requires separate destination-side survey and traversal evidence.

- Maps age because topology measurements and route updates must themselves be physically carried.

> *The nearest star is not necessarily your nearest neighbor.*

## 6.5 The Nine Loom Principles

These are deep design axioms. They constrain future writing and GM behavior; they are not nine “laws” publicly agreed upon by 2226 physicists.

### 1. Local Physical Closure

Within an ordinary projected domain, events have physically describable causes. The deeper cosmology never licenses arbitrary magic or “the Loom wanted it.”

### 2. Bounded Projection

Every observer, sensor, model, and local system accesses only a bounded part of the total physical state. No one has a God’s-eye data feed.

### 3. Relational Actualization

Some physical properties are most fundamentally meaningful through interactions and relationships rather than as exhaustive context-free catalogues.

### 4. Temporal Serialization

Finite agents experience causally ordered sequences even if deeper reality does not contain one universal moving present.

### 5. Dynamical Recurrence

Similar dynamics can repeatedly settle into analogous structures and attractors. Recurring forms do not require supernatural Platonic templates.

### 6. Recursive Causal Incorporation

Representations become causally meaningful when a system models information and acts differently because of the model. Meaning can matter physically without escaping physics.

### 7. Topological Correspondence

Events far apart in projected spacetime may share deeper relational ancestry without exchanging a controllable signal.

### 8. Perspectival Synergy

Independent bounded observations can jointly constrain the underlying state far better than any single perspective.

### 9. Constrained Agency

Agents select among physically admissible actions using internal models. Agency is causal at the organism/model level without requiring a violation of physical law.

## 6.6 The translation domain and lattice

A Loom vessel forms a coherent bounded translation domain D around the ship. A distributed lattice establishes extremely precise relational/metric boundary conditions around the domain. The lattice is not the reactor and not the torch. It is a life-limited precision system fed by stored energy and controlled by metrology and computation.

<img src="assets/foundational/media/image8.png" style="width:5.8in;height:0.88217in" />

Illustrative lattice-coherence measure. High Q means the boundary behaves as one coherent engineered system.

<img src="assets/foundational/media/image9.png" style="width:5.8in;height:0.43044in" />

A compact schematic of translation viability; not a complete 2226 field equation.

- Mass, translated boundary area, mass distribution, local geometry, destination coherence, lattice condition, stored energy, thermal state, and desired momentum mapping all matter.

- Compact vessels pay less translated-volume penalty than sprawling freighters.

- Cargo can change the solution. “Can fit through the hatch” is not equivalent to “inside the certified translation envelope.”

- The small-ship advantage is real but not magical: a small vessel has less domain to control and may also have less thermal reserve, redundancy, or payload.

## 6.7 Translation is not ordinary motion

<img src="assets/foundational/media/image10.png" style="width:5.8in;height:0.80105in" />

A translation maps one coherent local domain state to another; it is not a worldline through intervening light-years.

There is no meaningful macroscopic state “halfway to Tau Ceti.” A routine translation nevertheless takes finite local proper time because the field must form, cross commitment, resolve, relax, and reacquire ordinary external geometry.

| **Phase**     | **Physical meaning**                                                                     | **Typical operational experience**                                                      |
|---------------|------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Formation     | Lattice establishes the relational boundary and charges toward a viable solution.        | Minutes or longer; ship remains conventionally observable but increasingly distinctive. |
| Commitment    | The solution crosses a point beyond which simple safe collapse is no longer available.   | Very short; modern systems are conservative about crossing it.                          |
| Transition    | Local domain changes relational embedding; no intervening ordinary location exists.      | Often sub-second in working design language; external scene becomes undefined.          |
| Relaxation    | Field energy and geometry settle into the destination-local state.                       | Seconds to minutes; thermal/electrical transients and checks.                           |
| Reacquisition | External stars/sensors become meaningful and navigation establishes the actual endpoint. | “STELLAR FIX CONFIRMED” is the operational proof that arrival matches the model.        |

### 6.7.1 Measurement horizon

Near transition, ordinary external electromagnetic observations cease to correspond cleanly to one macroscopic outside scene. Cameras do not fail; “the outside” is temporarily not represented by a normal single local sky. Internal clocks, hull instrumentation, lattice metrology, crew physiology, quantum references, and field sensors continue recording.

## 6.8 Momentum, energy, and arrival state

<img src="assets/foundational/media/image11.png" style="width:5.8in;height:0.80105in" />

Illustrative four-momentum mapping. Deliberately engineered arrival-state change is represented by Δp and must be physically paid for.

The Loom is not free braking. The lowest-cost solution maps the vessel’s physical state into the destination neighborhood according to the admissible relationship. A navigator can deliberately request a different arrival state, but the energy and field budget must account for the difference. Preferred departure vectors and translation windows therefore exist without requiring literal gates.

<img src="assets/foundational/media/image12.png" style="width:5.8in;height:0.80105in" />

No closed sequence of Loom translations, regardless of leg count, has produced net extractable energy after all physical accounts are included.

| **LOCKED CANON** Lattice geometry can change how energy and momentum are mapped. It cannot substitute for the physical budget needed to create a net energy or momentum gain. A→B→C→A does not become a perpetual-motion machine. |
|-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

## 6.9 Causality and Chronology Exclusion

<img src="assets/foundational/media/image13.png" style="width:5.8in;height:0.80105in" />

GM deep-model rule: the simulated causal ordering is acyclic.

Humanity phrases the evidence more cautiously. No experimentally realized Loom solution has created a closed causal loop, and all known solution families that would imply one fail before stable commitment. Researchers can model many forbidden configurations but do not know why the deeper structure has this property.

| **GM-ONLY DEEP MODEL** The simulation does not permit actual closed causal loops. Attempts do not receive a supernatural “NO TIME TRAVEL” message; they fail through ordinary solution divergence, inability to reach commitment, safe abort, or other physically modeled off-nominal behavior. |
|-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

> *Metric distance can be bypassed. Causal ordering cannot.*

An observed discrepancy between ship proper time and an independently established external epoch is not automatically “time travel” and is not a selectable navigation capability. If a campaign establishes such a chronology anomaly, record the ship’s proper-time history, the external epoch estimate, and the causal offset separately; preserve provenance; and treat the mechanism as unresolved unless a later canon revision explains it. No chronology anomaly licenses a closed loop, backward targeting, or a controllable temporal communications channel.

## 6.10 Reciprocity, directionality, and return risk

Measured Loom coherence appears fundamentally reciprocal to a good approximation, but engineering accessibility is not. Local origin geometry, destination geometry, vessel domain, lattice capability, power, thermal state, requested momentum mapping, and uncertainty can make A→B practical while B→A lies outside the vessel’s certified or physical envelope.

Operational maps therefore store direction-specific route solutions and effective route burden \(\chi_{A\rightarrow B}\). This is an engineering statement, not proof that the underlying relational structure is ontologically a one-way graph.

> *One successful crossing proves you can get there. It does not prove you can come home.*

## 6.11 Geometric Admissibility and destination compatibility

The old shorthand “strong gravity prevents Loom” is too crude. Translation viability depends on the geometry of the entire domain and endpoint state: curvature, curvature gradients and tidal shear, stress-energy and matter density, electromagnetic conditions, causal structure, domain size, Loom coherence, lattice coherence, and uncertainty.

<img src="assets/foundational/media/image14.png" style="width:5.8in;height:0.71482in" />

Geometric Admissibility is a multivariable condition, not a single “gravity-well” stat.

A coherent domain cannot resolve into a macroscopically occupied incompatible destination domain. Navigation therefore targets broad low-density emergence volumes. Predictable conflicts prevent commitment; late-discovered extreme conflicts can force displacement, reversion, or domain failure. Ships do not materialize inside ships, planets, stations, or Manhattan.

| **LOCKED CANON** Translation is one-to-one at the domain level. No observed event has produced two coherent outputs of the same physical domain. Matter duplication is not an allowed exploit. |
|------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

## 6.12 Failure taxonomy

Commercial Loom travel is viable because most problems occur before commitment and simply abort. Serious events are rare; unresolved events are rarer still.

A **mis-translation** is not an arbitrarily large Cartesian navigation error. The committed domain resolves through a different physically admissible relationship than the intended one. Ordinary emergence displacement remains error around the realized endpoint. The exact mechanism by which a failed solution selects an alternate admissible relationship is not locked canon. Chronology displacement is not an automatic property of mis-translation.

| **Class**                | **Description**                                                                             | **What is known**                                                                        |
|--------------------------|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Pre-commitment abort     | coherence, geometry, power, thermal, destination, or sensor confidence falls outside limits | Routine engineering event; no translation occurs.                                        |
| Reversion                | post-commitment state resolves back toward origin                                           | Can damage lattice/ship; still locally recoverable.                                      |
| Destination displacement | correct relationship but outside intended emergence volume                                  | Potentially dangerous; endpoint must be re-established.                                  |
| Mis-translation          | resolves through another admissible relationship                                            | Extremely rare on mature routes; higher risk in experimental conditions.                 |
| Domain decoherence       | translation domain loses physical integrity after commitment                                | Structural, thermal, neurological, or fatal consequences; not a neat split across stars. |
| Non-return               | no subsequent observation establishes outcome                                               | Do not equate absence with confirmed destruction. A small number remain unresolved.      |

## 6.13 Translation-Associated Altered Consciousness (TAAC)

Most people experience only brief vertigo, spatial mismatch, time distortion, or “that felt strange.” A minority experience intense altered states phenomenologically resembling powerful psychedelic, mystical, near-death, or dissociative experiences. This is a real medical phenomenon. Its meaning is not established.

- recursive or apparently impossible geometry

- ego dissolution or loss of body boundaries

- subjective durations far longer than the measured transition

- overwhelming connectedness or scale

- communication experienced without language

- apparent encounters with agency, presence, or intelligence

- a conviction of profound understanding that cannot be fully reconstructed afterward

| **LOCKED CANON** Perceived agency does not establish external agency. No verified Loom experience has demonstrated controllable contact with an entity, deity, alien, dead person, or future self. |
|----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

## 6.14 Translation-Associated Anomalous Memory (TAAM)

Rarely, especially after unusual or severe transitions, people report memories experienced as ordinary autobiography that cannot be reconciled with observable history: a room never visited, a different relationship, an argument with an unknown person, a childhood event nobody else remembers, or something as mundane as a coffee cup used for twenty years that never existed.

The leading scientific explanation is reconstructive-memory disruption under unusual neurophysical conditions. The problem is that prospective, cryptographically timestamped studies have left a tiny, controversial residual of unusually specific later correspondences after many obvious biases are controlled.

| **Class**                            | **Meaning**                                                                                                                        |
|--------------------------------------|------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| I — coincidence                      | Ordinary coincidence. Almost everything belongs here.                                                                              |
| II — personally compelling           | Specific enough to disturb the experiencer but statistically uninteresting.                                                        |
| III — anomalous correspondence       | Prospectively recorded and individually unusual enough for serious investigation.                                                  |
| IV — persistent statistical residual | Extremely rare population-level residual that survives some serious controls but is model- and method-sensitive. Cause unresolved. |

> *Write it down before you talk to anyone.*

That phrase is ordinary spacer folk practice after a strange crossing: record first, discuss later, because discussion contaminates evidence.

### 6.14.1 Correlation without channel capacity

The anomaly cannot be addressed, selected, or encoded. Attempts to impose red/blue, bit, semantic, large-N, machine-learning, or synthetic-mind payload protocols collapse to chance. Whatever residual exists appears in uncontrolled correspondence rather than controllable communication.

<img src="assets/foundational/media/image15.png" style="width:5.8in;height:0.66939in" />

Operationally, anomalous outputs carry approximately zero controllable mutual information about a chosen message.

| **LOCKED CANON** TAAM and synchronicity cannot be turned into FTL Morse code, prophecy-on-demand, or a puzzle-solving oracle. Synchronicities are residue, not quest hints. |
|-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

## 6.15 Post-Translation Continuity Disorder

A rare syndrome, strongly associated with severe off-nominal events, leaves an otherwise functional person with intact memory and identity records who nonetheless insists: “The person who entered the Loom is not the person who came out.” Medicine treats this as neuropsychiatric pathology; Patternists and Continuists argue over the interpretation. No evidence establishes actual destruction or replacement of consciousness.

Translation psychiatry is a legitimate interdisciplinary field spanning neurology, psychiatry, aerospace medicine, cognitive science, and Loom exposure medicine. Post-transition cognitive capture is medically useful and politically explosive because employers, insurers, militaries, and courts want access to it.

## 6.16 Synthetic minds and translation

Synthetic systems generally do not report human-style TAAM. Their explicit stored records remain internally consistent, which strongly supports neurological explanations. Disputed cases exist in which persistent synthetic agents report an internal expectation they cannot provenance: “I expected three people on the bridge.” No corrupted file or prediction explains the expectation. The cases are rare and may not be one phenomenon.

Sending synthetic probes first is rational and standard. If the probe’s intelligence is legally recognized as a person, however, “fork one and treat the lost branch as disposable” becomes a civil-rights question rather than a free risk bypass.

## 6.17 Strong-field Loom physics

Black holes, neutron stars, compact-object mergers, and other extreme environments are not magical Loom portals. They are laboratories where the relationship between emergent spacetime and deeper topology can be tested under maximal stress.

### 6.17.1 Causally trapped domains

| **LOCKED CANON** No stable outward Loom translation has ever been demonstrated from a causally trapped domain. As trapped-surface conditions develop, admissible outward translation families collapse. The ship does not possess an omniscient “event horizon detector”; it measures local geometry and solution viability. |
|------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

This distinction matters because an event horizon is a global causal structure, while operational spacecraft must work with local and quasi-local observables. Researchers compare Loom-solution collapse against apparent, trapping, and dynamical horizons and against globally reconstructed event horizons after the fact. Inward solutions can remain mathematically admissible longer than outward ones; that does not imply a rescue path.

### 6.17.2 Horizon topology spectroscopy

Loom metrology around black holes tests whether relational-coherence behavior tracks ordinary strong-field models exactly. Tiny persistent residuals around particular masses/spins would be scientifically revolutionary without proving any metaphysical interpretation. “Horizon topology spectroscopy” is a useful working field label, not a guarantee that every black hole produces an anomaly.

### 6.17.3 Topological scars

Certain classes of extreme gravitational history — including some compact-object mergers — are statistically associated with persistent deviations from otherwise successful Loom-topology models. The relationship is not deterministic. Some mergers leave no measurable scar; some anomalous regions have no known merger history; some apparent correlations disappear when the astrophysical reconstruction improves.

> *The Loom graph has a past.*

### 6.17.4 Gravitational-wave metrology

Dynamical spacetime changes can perturb Loom observables. Near extreme events, route confidence or solution families may transiently change; at great distance the effect is primarily metrological. Loom instrumentation therefore becomes an additional gravitational-wave modality rather than a source of arbitrary route weather.

## 6.18 Wormholes, dark matter, dark energy, neutrinos, and tachyons

| **Topic**                            | **LOOM 2226 treatment**                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
|--------------------------------------|-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Wormholes                            | Loom translation is not a wormhole network: no throat, tunnel, or traversed interior is required. Serious theorists may argue that wormhole mathematics and Loom topology are different emergent manifestations of deeper relational structure.                                           |
| Dark matter                          | Dark matter is not “Loom substance.” Loom metrology can provide an independent tomography of gravitational structure and galactic halos without identifying the particle/field nature of dark matter.                                                                                     |
| Dark energy                          | Not Loom fuel. It may eventually prove relevant to very-long-timescale evolution of relational topology, but this is cosmological theory rather than routine navigation.                                                                                                                  |
| Neutrinos / multimessenger astronomy | Neutrinos are instrumentation and continuity probes, not magic carriers. Astrophysical neutrino timing can cross-check violent events; precision particle-state experiments support physical state continuity through translation. No universal intrinsic Loom-neutrino flash is assumed. |
| Tachyons                             | No verified tachyon particle and no need for one. Loom does not vindicate tachyonic signalling. Journalists still say “tachyon drive”; physicists still hate it.                                                                                                                          |

## 6.19 State continuity through translation

Before crew certification, researchers translated atomic clocks, unstable isotopes, prepared quantum states, biological samples, synthetic processes, particle experiments, and every other precision reference they could reasonably devise. Within experimental limits, the bounded domain preserves its physical state-history rather than being destructively scanned and reconstructed at the destination.

| **LOCKED CANON** Translation is not a Star Trek transporter. There is no physical evidence that routine translation kills the original and manufactures a copy. This does not solve the philosophical question of subjective continuity. |
|------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

## 6.20 What remains unknown

| **INTENTIONALLY UNRESOLVED** The design does not settle whether the relational substrate is ultimately fundamental; whether consciousness has privileged access to topology; whether perceived entities correspond to anything external; why the Class IV residual exists; whether non-return cases share a cause; whether consciousness persists after biological death; whether synthetic and biological minds differ metaphysically; why Chronology Exclusion exists; or whether anything can “notice” humanity manipulating the deeper structure. |
|-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

These are not all clues to one future reveal. Some may remain unanswered forever. The setting is allowed to contain several unrelated mysteries, ordinary mistakes, frauds, and human projections alongside any genuine anomaly.

## 6.21 The Strangeness Problem

LOCKED CANON: Over roughly two centuries, better instruments, ubiquitous authenticated recording, synthetic analysis, advanced neuroscience, metric engineering, and finally Loom experimentation have caused a small class of anomalies to survive explanations that once disposed of them. The setting does not assume that these residuals share a cause, or even that the rate of genuine anomaly is constant in time.

Most apparent high strangeness still resolves normally: fraud, sensor artifacts, contamination, misunderstood biology, classified technology, selection effects, bad statistics, memory reconstruction, ordinary astrophysics, or human pattern-seeking. The important historical change is signal-to-noise. Better provenance and better models eliminate enormous amounts of bullshit; the residue therefore becomes harder, not easier, to ignore.

### 6.21.1 Physical residuals

Examples include correlated detector transients with no identified common source, metric or gravitational residuals that survive improved modeling, persistent topological-scar anomalies, unusual coherence behavior near compact objects, and rare multi-instrument events in which independent systems register a common disturbance but disagree about its physical category. None has produced controllable FTL signaling, free energy, or a verified alien explanation.

### 6.21.2 Consciousness residuals

Advanced neuroscience explains a great deal of historical mystical, psychedelic, near-death, dissociative, and anomalous-memory material. It also leaves small contested tails: recurring phenomenological structures, paired or clustered reports slightly more similar than expected, unprovenanced expectations, continuity disturbances, and the later TAAC/TAAM datasets. Shared brain architecture, reconstruction, and cultural priming remain powerful explanations.

### 6.21.3 Cross-domain events

The most controversial cases are mixed-domain events in which an unusual cognitive report and an independently measured physical anomaly occur in the same narrow interval. Most claimed cases fail replication or dissolve under better controls. A small number remain famous precisely because no accepted model completely explains both sides. Grouping them as one phenomenon is itself scientifically disputed.

### 6.21.4 Historical arc

Early 21st-century high-strangeness evidence was mostly anecdotal or instrumentally weak. By 2050–2100 authenticated sensor networks and AI forensics collapsed many old anomaly categories. From roughly 2100 onward, advanced neuroscience and synthetic cognition created controlled datasets about subjective experience. The Multiplicity era made identity and continuity engineering questions rather than purely philosophical ones. Metric engineering added deliberate spacetime manipulation. Loom translation then created a repeatable laboratory in which ordinary external spacetime description, deep relational metrology, and unusual conscious experience overlap.

### 6.21.5 Competing explanations

- Measurement Hypothesis — nothing became stranger; humanity became capable of detecting rare phenomena.

- Selection Hypothesis — increasingly competent filters remove mundane cases, leaving a residue selected for weirdness.

- Technological Access Hypothesis — metric and Loom technology expose physical regimes humans previously never occupied.

- Historical Continuity Hypothesis — some older anomalies were manifestations of the same broad classes, but past humans lacked adequate concepts or instrumentation.

- Perturbation Hypothesis — human manipulation of relational structure is itself changing the observed anomaly landscape. This remains highly speculative and unproven.

### 6.21.6 High-strangeness firewall

- Anomalies do not converge automatically. There is no GM assumption that every mystery shares one cause.

- Relational ontology does not imply universal consciousness.

- Shared relational ancestry does not imply shared identity.

- Correlation does not imply communication.

- Physical dependence does not imply cognitive control.

- Emergence does not imply illusion. An emergent person is still a person.

- Old UAP, mystical, paranormal, or anomalous records may acquire new questions under later analysis; they do not become retroactively validated extraordinary claims.

GM RULE: The setting does not decide whether the genuine anomaly rate is increasing. It may be measurement, selection, access to new physical regimes, long-cycle astrophysical structure, interaction with intelligent complexity, or something not yet imagined. “Something noticed” is an available possibility, never a default answer.

# 7. Spacecraft Engineering, Maintenance & Certification

Ship systems expose real tradeoffs. The simulation does not collapse fuel, reaction mass, stored energy, heat, coolant, calibration, component life, and institutional confidence into one generic resource because they create different decisions.

## 7.1 Energy and reactor architecture
