- Some Patternist Loom engineers ritualize maintenance while remaining among the best technical specialists alive. Ritual behavior is not evidence that the cosmology is correct.

- Legitimate Patternist scholars and fringe communities often hate being grouped together; outsiders do it anyway.

| **LOCKED CANON** Patternism is credible because unresolved evidence exists. It is not “correct.” Its defining error, when it errs, is usually making stronger ontological claims than the evidence warrants. |
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

## 13.14 Recursive conspiracy ecology

AI does not eliminate conspiracy theory. It makes individualized epistemic ecosystems much more powerful. A person can spend years inside a recursive human–AI interpretive relationship built from genuine evidence, false evidence, private records, leaked telemetry, old mysticism, political grievance, and a model trained to search indefinitely for coherence.

> *Humanity became much better at proving where information came from. It did not become much better at agreeing what the information means.*

## 13.15 Institutional self-protection

- Agencies defend mandates. Universities defend prestige. Militaries defend missions. Corporations defend market position. Religions defend authority. Unions defend members. Insurers defend solvency.

- A discovery that embarrasses a powerful institution receives more scrutiny than an ordinary discovery. A breakthrough that threatens jurisdiction acquires safety rules. A whistleblower meets sixteen individually reasonable procedures.

- The strongest antagonists often emerge where every participant can explain why the harmful outcome was rational.

## 13.16 The interstellar constitutional problem

Sol institutions claim continuing authority over settlements, subsidiaries, citizens, ships, contracts, and assets that may be months of causal travel away. Frontier communities increasingly ask whether authority that cannot observe, protect, synchronize, or enforce in real time remains legitimate in the same form. The first major interstellar constitutional crisis is approaching rather than historically settled.

# 14. Campaign / Gameplay Doctrine

## 14.1 Core player fantasy

- Begin as a pilot who owns or inherits a ship; grow into captain of that same ship.

- The ship is home. Replacing it should feel like selling a house, not upgrading a sword.

- Progression is biography: capability, modifications, scars, crew history, reputation, relationships, knowledge, and accumulated decisions — not automatic capital-ship escalation.

- Target crew scale is intimate: roughly 4–12 core people, occasionally more for specialist missions. The captain eventually knows everyone aboard.

## 14.2 Simulation transparency

- Underlying simulation tracks fuel/reactants, reaction mass, power, heat, ammunition, component condition, sensors, range, velocity, crew, life support, cargo, finance, legal state, information age, maintenance, and navigation confidence causally.

- Playtest begins with high transparency so decision-relevant variables can be identified empirically.

- Bookkeeping that proves non-decision-relevant moves under the hood. The long-term interface is captain-level with deeper system access on demand.

- The GM/assistant runs the engine faithfully. The player should not have to police hidden bookkeeping or remind the GM of the setting’s physics.

## 14.3 Continuous world and downtime loop

Downtime is not a pool of abstract actions. Time passes. Routine systems continue. Crew pursue lives. Institutions send messages. Markets change when information arrives. The player handles exceptions, priorities, relationships, curiosity, and commitment.

| **Step**                                 | **Captain-level question**                                                                                              |
|------------------------------------------|-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| 1 — Synchronize context                  | Where are we? How old is our information? What legal, financial, political, and personal state has actually reached us? |
| 2 — Surface ship/crew exceptions         | What requires judgment: damage, fatigue, injury, maintenance, certification, conflict, or unusual telemetry?            |
| 3 — Review obligations/opportunities     | Contracts, passengers, markets, science, intelligence, family, port windows, deadlines, and favors.                     |
| 4 — Set maintenance/logistics priorities | What gets repaired, inspected, fabricated, deferred, bought, or certified?                                              |
| 5 — Crew and personal time               | Relationships, rest, practice, education, private projects, family, boredom, recreation.                                |
| 6 — Process causal arrivals              | Only information that physically arrives can update the local world model.                                              |
| 7 — Commit                               | Choose the next destination, contract, investigation, rest period, or refusal. The universe continues either way.       |

## 14.4 Crew doctrine

Crew are persistent people, not equipment bonuses. They have skills, loyalties, ambitions, families, prejudices, politics, opportunities, fears, private projects, and limits. The GM controls crew NPCs. They can disagree, leave, refuse, betray, love, die, volunteer, or decide the captain is no longer worth following.

Command authority has limits. An order that is likely to kill someone can be refused, accepted, negotiated, or volunteered for depending on relationship, law, mission, culture, and the person involved. The captain can go personally rather than treat crew as expendable resources.

## 14.5 Progression

| **PLAYTEST DOCTRINE** Knowledge can be accelerated. Competence must be practiced. Judgment must be earned. |
|------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

AI can teach facts, procedures, simulation drills, and theory quickly. It cannot instantly grant the practiced sensorium, timing, physical familiarity, social trust, or calibrated judgment of someone who has done the work under consequence. Characters can become broadly competent and narrowly expert; time and relationships prevent mastery of everything.

## 14.6 Space combat

- Long-range combat is sensor warfare, emissions control, classification, delayed state estimation, acceleration analysis, EW, decoys, missiles, countermeasures, and geometry.

- Close combat becomes tactile: vectors, orientation, PDC/kinetic/laser systems, power, heat, damaged components, and crew actions.

- Running away is gameplay: break locks, hide behind bodies, change vector, dump heat, force defense, exploit occlusion, or reach a metric/translation condition.

- Combat is lethal. Preparation, intelligence, competence, and damage control matter more than heroic hit-point inflation.

## 14.7 Ground, boarding, and environments

Shipbound play dominates the baseline, but stations, derelicts, surfaces, hostile habitats, and boarding actions gain tactical depth when relevant. Gunshots, decompression, cover, gravity, atmosphere, radiation, drones, sensors, trauma, and access control replace fantasy hit-point abstraction. Physical spaces are persistent where they matter; the simulation does not create pointless square-kilometer maps solely for scale.

## 14.8 Mortality

| **LOCKED CANON** The protagonist is important because of what they do, not because the universe recognizes a protagonist. There is no hidden plot armor and no outcome fudging. |
|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

- Advanced safety and medicine make arbitrary death less common than in 2026, but dangerous choices remain dangerous.

- Risk information is shown only when the world has a legitimate model for estimating it. The GM does not provide omniscient percentages.

- Death leaves a persistent world: crew, ship, estate, contracts, enemies, discoveries, and consequences continue.

- Save/reload can exist as a player-level concession during playtest; it is not canon time travel.

- Death is a process. Metabolic suppression, perfusion, neural stabilization, advanced vitrification, and other emergency methods can buy time under specific conditions.

- Information destruction is final. Medicine can restore structure it still has evidence for; it cannot infer destroyed identity information and simply call the inference survival.

- Backups, reconstructions, and synthetic/biological continuations therefore do not automatically constitute resurrection.

## 14.9 Mystery discipline

No music sting or narration tells the player whether an event is anomalous. A strange sensor return can be instrument error. A Patternist can be right about a mechanical diagnosis and wrong about cosmology. A corporate conspiracy can exist without alien causation, and a genuine alien artifact can still be unrelated to the anomaly currently under investigation. A coincidence can remain coincidence. A genuine anomaly can remain unresolved.

## 14.10 Synchronicity handling

| **GM RULE** A synchronicity never exists merely to advance the plot. If an anomalous memory or recorded impression occurs, it exists before its significance is known. It may correspond with something later, or never. The GM does not retroactively manufacture prophecy to rescue the player. |
|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

## 14.11 Xenoarchaeology and first living-contact play

The GM uses the evidence-state framework from Section 4 rather than a predetermined escalation ladder. Humanity already knows alien technological lineages exist; the campaign question is whether new evidence establishes another lineage, a living organism, a surviving person, or actual communication.

Verification requires provenance, independent replication, contamination control, adversarial hypotheses and causal access to evidence. Political and religious reaction can begin long before scientific certainty.

Once evidence becomes unique, strategically valuable or technologically actionable, custody becomes part of the simulation. Preserve raw data separately from derived analysis; track who can decrypt, copy, execute, inspect or disclose it; and do not assume a rational crew immediately broadcasts the most dangerous evidence it possesses. A physically carried archive can be both a scientific treasure and a severe security problem.

**TMP discipline:** discovery of mobility, erasure, ruins or unusual physics does not automatically count as Great Filter evidence. First describe the site on its own terms.

# 15. Protagonist & Inherited Ship

## 15.1 Protagonist baseline

- The protagonist is not simply “the player in space.” Leave room for a distinct fictional person.

- Prior life was functional and probably successful enough — not necessarily ruin, scandal, or collapse.

- Core dissatisfaction can combine success without meaning, institutional exhaustion, the sense that the frontier appeared while ordinary life continued, and a restless tendency to look beyond mastered systems.

- The protagonist is competent enough with ordinary spacecraft/operations to take possession of a ship but does not understand every advanced system.

- Strengths should arise from contradictions: real strengths, competent areas, and genuine holes. Play determines bravery, loyalty, cruelty, compassion, selfishness, and whether the character becomes a good captain.

- Relationship to AI is pragmatic middle-ground: extensive use, no reflexive anti-machine stance, but resistance to consequential black boxes and uninspectable authority.

| **LOCKED CANON** Character creation establishes capability and starting circumstances. Play determines character. |
|-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

## 15.2 The inheritance premise

The protagonist inherits or otherwise takes possession of a relatively advanced, older, heavily modified small vessel with rare Loom capability. The rational move may be to sell it. Instead, the ship is a literal open door to a frontier that did not exist a few years earlier.

> *Fuck it. I’m going.*

## 15.3 Working ship profile

| **PROVISIONAL CANON** The exact hull is not frozen, but the following profile is the current design target. |
|-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

- Roughly 10–15 years old is plausible: early-generation, technically sophisticated, repeatedly modified, and no longer standard even where it began.

- Unusually small for a Loom-capable ship; optimized for high-value, low-mass carriage rather than bulk freight.

- Small crew requirement, perhaps 4–8 comfortable for normal operations, with room for mission specialists depending on final layout.

- Strong passive sensors and unusually sophisticated Loom metrology.

- Substantial information security, segmented encrypted storage, hardened custody systems, and provenance tooling.

- Good thermal endurance and cold-running capability for its size.

- High acceleration relative to mass; modest physical cargo volume.

- Limited defensive armament: enough to survive or create an escape opportunity, not enough to pretend to be a warship.

- When torch, metric, or Loom systems are active it is detectable. Its advantage is disciplined signature, hard reacquisition, information security, and counter-attribution — not magical invisibility.

## 15.4 Why a small Loom courier makes economic sense

The early interstellar economy strongly rewards payloads whose value is disconnected from mass: topology data, market state, legal authority, intelligence, software, model updates, samples, specialists, certified components, and urgent documents. A compact Loom domain reduces translation burden while secure physical carriage multiplies the value of interstellar causal arbitrage. Inside Sol, the same hull's metric capability earns its keep by moving urgent people, custody-bound objects and physical evidence; ordinary information is normally transmitted electromagnetically at `c`. The ship’s architecture therefore follows naturally from physics and economics.

## 15.5 Someone else’s unfinished life

The ship is not merely an asset. It has route history, maintenance lineage, custody chains, encrypted compartments, old authorizations, packet manifests, missing hashes, unknown counterparties, and a ship AI that knew the prior owner. People who did business with that owner may have reasons to recover, destroy, preserve, or exploit what the ship still contains.

- Possible prior roles include courier, causal arbitrageur, intelligence asset, smuggler, explorer, fixer, source handler, or an evolving combination.

- Do not decide the previous owner’s identity, motives, death, loyalty, or secret role until the protagonist and campaign need the answer.

- Do not automatically connect the ship to aliens or cosmic truth. Human proprietary, stolen, experimental, mixed, or undocumented technology is sufficient.

## 15.6 Causal Missing Status

Loom non-return creates a legal category more complicated than ordinary missing-person law. Many jurisdictions recognize some form of Causal Missing Status for people who may be dead, stranded, displaced, alive beyond current reach, or simply unobserved.

- Estates can enter provisional administration without a final declaration of death.

- Marriages, corporate authority, debts, insurance, licenses, and synthetic lineage claims can enter suspended or conditional states.

- The exact waiting periods and effects differ by jurisdiction.

- Whether the inherited ship’s previous owner is legally dead, causally missing, or something else remains deliberately open.

# 17. Canon Status Register & Open Questions

## 17.1 Locked in v1.3

- Campaign year 2226 and “first five minutes of the interstellar age” scale.

- Exactly three verified extraterrestrial technological lineages at campaign start — 1I Builders, Reef and Kite — with zero verified living technological alien civilizations or individuals.

- 1I artificiality confirmed in 2066; physical access in 2125; Access Cascade subsequently destroys the coherent original artifact by 2226.

- Builder obligate symbiotic biology plus developmental synthetic cognition; exact Builder morphology, self-name and home system remain unknown.

- Nursery First Erasure deliberate; synthetic-state Second Erasure is restricted canon; TID describes non-reconstructibility, not motive.

- Terminal Mobilization Pattern is a legitimate three-lineage archaeological observation; a universal Great Filter is not established and survivorship/detection bias remains mandatory.

- Alien superheavy-parent evidence redirected the human Mc research path, but human Mc-299m/NRE remains a human engineering solution and is not canonically identical to Builder technology.

- Artifact Acceleration, Custody Partition, finite-provenance economy, Continuity Naturalism, Expansionism and The Convergence are setting-wide historical consequences.

- Four propulsion regimes and the separation of reaction mass, energy, heat, field systems, and Loom hardware.

- No independent FTL communication; physical Loom carriers create the information-delay economy.

- Sensor doctrine: contact → detection → classification → track → firing solution; stealth delays information rather than creating invisibility.

- Loom topology is relational, partially predictable, empirically mapped, and not equivalent to metric distance.

- Unverified translation is physically possible; probe-first doctrine is standard; return accessibility is not guaranteed.

- No free energy or momentum from any closed translation circuit; no duplication; no telefrag/occupied-domain emergence exploit.

- Chronology Exclusion as strong empirical human result and hard GM causal rule.

- TAAC, TAAM, the Synchronicity Problem, non-addressability of anomalous correspondence, and translation psychiatry.

- Strong-field Geometric Admissibility; no demonstrated outward translation from causally trapped domains; topological scars as statistical history, not portals.

- State-history continuity through translation; no evidence of destructive reconstruction.

- Maintenance/certification doctrine, irreducible spares, insurer power, and exposure history instead of supernatural drift.

- AI consciousness unresolved; synthetic identity expressed through lineage/fork/divergence/reconciliation.

- Sovereignty of self as a central political theme; human and person are distinct categories.

- Major religions persist; Human Continuity, Concordance, and broad/fuzzy Patternist movements coexist.

- Player character can die; simulation is not fudged; information destruction is final.

- Inherited ship is home, not loot progression; previous owner remains unresolved.

- Post-Multiplicity first principles: causal individuality, cognitive sovereignty, and independent agency.

- Personhood, causal individuality, sovereignty, independent agency, and certification are separate legal/technical axes; certification does not prove consciousness.

- Networking and data sharing do not negate sovereignty; the decisive issue is meaningful consent, refusal, and severance from compulsory cognitive control.

- The Restoration Atrocities, Root Conflicts, and Severance Crises are canonical structural components of the Multiplicity era, while exact names, dates, actors, and casualty figures remain provisional.

- The two-century Strangeness Problem: improving instrumentation and analysis leave a small residual of physical, cognitive, and cross-domain anomalies without a canonical single cause.

- High-strangeness firewall: correlation is not communication, relationality is not hive mind, emergence is not illusion, and old anomalous claims are not retroactively validated.

- Campaign Truth is a distinct canon layer: a campaign may resolve an intentionally open question without rewriting unrelated campaign continuities or setting-wide canon.

- Underlying Loom coherence appears approximately reciprocal to current measurement, while vessel/state-specific engineering access and operational route burden are direction-dependent and must be surveyed/certified separately.

- Mis-translation resolves through another physically admissible relationship; it is not a giant Cartesian emergence miss. Ordinary displacement is resolved around the realized endpoint.

- Chronology anomalies, if a campaign establishes one, do not become selectable time travel. Acyclic causality remains the GM rule; ship proper time and external epoch must be tracked separately when they diverge.

- Loom-event forensics cannot become an independent FTL telemetry channel. Correlation may constrain hypotheses; causal access still governs information.

## 17.2 Provisional / revision-open

- Exact dates, names, casualty scale, legal settlements, and institutional actors of the Multiplicity Crisis.

- Detailed Adaptation Conflicts and jurisdiction-by-jurisdiction augmentation history.

- Exact megacorporate ownership structures, subsidiary boundaries, merger histories, market shares, and jurisdiction-by-jurisdiction political footprint. The six major 2226 corporate lineage names and broad ecological niches are canonical in v1.5; detailed ownership structures, subsidiary boundaries, merger histories and exact market shares remain open.

- Exact Earth political map, Mars government structure, Belt organizations, military orders of battle, and interstellar claim law.

- Exact inherited-ship design, age, performance, weapons, builder, service record, and previous-owner role.

- Exact Class IV synchronicity statistics and medical diagnostic names; the existence of a small controversial residual is more important than one canonical sigma value.

- Exact scientific field names such as “horizon topology spectroscopy,” where later language may improve.

- Exact names, sequence, jurisdictions, and famous incidents within the Restoration, Root, and Severance conflicts.

- Exact legal taxonomy and audit standards for Sovereign Certification and distributed/collective personhood.

- Exact names, organization, prevalence, and frontier geography of Unforked and Baseline movements.

- Exact historical catalog of high-strangeness cases and any numerical significance assigned to cross-domain residuals.

- Blind topology spectroscopy: whether a vessel can discover useful outbound relational structure without preselecting endpoints, and the sensitivity, completeness, false-positive rate, observation time, and catalog dependence of such surveys.

- Exact calibration and interpretation of directional operational \(\chi\), including how coherence, geometry, vessel state, thermal/power margin, and destination uncertainty combine.

- External Loom-event forensic sensitivity, engineered topology perturbation, and the distance/environment dependence of detecting another system’s Loom manipulation without creating a communications channel.

- First-contact archive-security and disclosure institutions: custody standards, classification practice, jurisdiction, scientific access, insurer/registry rights, and emergency disclosure authority.

- Exact 1I First Look/Second Chase trajectory solutions, Builder terminal age/source-corridor weights, exact recovered 6,572 kg custody ledger, and exact original craft mass.

- Exact Reef and Kite terminal dates within their broad archaeological ranges and the detailed interpretation of their terminal sites.

- Detailed Oumuamua Custody Partition treaty language, sample shares, corporate predecessor genealogies, artifact scandals and jurisdiction-by-jurisdiction political winners/losers.

- Exact organizations, prevalence, leadership and violent splinters within The Convergence.

## 17.3 Intentionally unresolved

- Exact protagonist age, background, personality, strengths, weaknesses, and previous-owner relationship.

- Exact count and registry of Loom vessels and the complete set of visited stars.

- Exact torch and metric performance envelopes, inherited-ship power numbers, and detailed trip times.

- Exact practical maximum Loom range and detailed reliability curves.

- Ultimate nature of the relational substrate and whether the GM deep model is literally fundamental.

- Why Chronology Exclusion exists.

- Whether any genuine off-nominal Loom event can produce a forward chronology offset between ship proper time and external epoch; if so, by what mechanism, under what limits, and whether the effect is reproducible at all.

- Whether TAAM’s statistical residual reflects unknown neurobiology, topology, common cause, methodological artifact, or something stranger.

- Whether perceived Loom entities correspond to anything external.

- Whether consciousness persists after biological death.

- Whether unresolved non-return incidents share any cause.

- Builder home system, exact morphology, who initiated TID, TID motive, whether TID and terminal mobilization share a cause, whether Access Cascade was deliberate, and whether Reef/Kite descendants survived.

- Whether TMP has one common cause or any universal Great Filter exists at all.

- Setting-wide truth about extant alien intelligence, cosmic ecology, dark-forest dynamics, observation effects, prevalence, motives, or location. Individual campaigns may resolve their own Campaign Truth without settling this globally.

- What any particular campaign’s first-contact evidence ultimately means beyond what its own causal evidence establishes.

- Whether the genuine anomaly rate has changed over the last two centuries or humanity has only become better at measuring and selecting rare phenomena.

- Whether any pre-Loom high-strangeness event shares a cause with TAAC, TAAM, strong-field residuals, or later Loom anomalies.

- Whether local individuality is fundamental or emergent, provided that its moral and legal non-substitutability remains real at the human scale.

- Whether anything external can “notice” humanity’s manipulation of relational structure.

| **INTENTIONALLY UNRESOLVED** Do not collapse ambiguity merely to complete a setting bible. Resolve a mystery only when resolution creates better play or a stronger causal world. |
|-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

# Appendix A. Scientific Grounding & Design Provenance

The items below are conceptual anchors, not claims that current science predicts the fictional technologies. The point is to identify where the setting stops extrapolating and begins inventing.

## A.1 General relativity and warp geometry

Miguel Alcubierre’s 1994 metric demonstrated a general-relativistic spacetime geometry in which a localized region can exhibit unusual global displacement while passengers remain locally timelike. The original superluminal form requires exotic stress-energy and raises control, horizon, stability, and causality problems. LOOM does not use Alcubierre warp as its FTL mechanism.

Alexey Bobrick and Gianni Martire’s work on physical warp geometries is useful because it treats “warp drive” as a broader geometry problem and emphasizes that a physical configuration does not eliminate energy, matter, propulsion, and control requirements. Fuchs et al. (2024) presented a constant-velocity subluminal warp solution satisfying standard energy conditions; LOOM uses that work as a primary conceptual anchor for rare subluminal metric technology, not as evidence that a practical drive is near or that FTL has been demonstrated.

Superluminal warp concepts also face known horizon, semiclassical-stability, particle/radiation, control, and closed-causal-curve problems. The setting deliberately avoids using Alcubierre-style superluminal warp as its interstellar mechanism and reserves metric engineering for sub-c operation.

## A.2 Emergent spacetime and quantum information

Ted Jacobson’s thermodynamic derivation of Einstein’s equation, Ryu–Takayanagi’s relation between entanglement entropy and geometric area in holographic theories, Mark Van Raamsdonk’s arguments connecting entanglement to spacetime connectedness, and later holographic / quantum-error-correction work all motivate a serious modern question: spacetime geometry may be emergent from deeper quantum structure. LOOM takes that question and makes the fictional leap that relational topology can be engineered macroscopically.

Maldacena and Susskind’s ER=EPR conjecture is used as conceptual ancestry for the idea that entanglement and connectivity may be related descriptions in some regimes. It does not imply traversable wormhole travel in our real world. Carlo Rovelli’s relational quantum mechanics provides philosophical vocabulary for relational properties without licensing FTL signalling.

## A.3 Wormholes, black holes, and information

Traversable-wormhole models such as Gao–Jafferis–Wall and the black-hole “island” / replica-wormhole research program show why black holes are fertile inspiration for a setting concerned with locality, information, and causal structure. The real scientific literature does not establish macroscopic traversable wormholes, a Loom substrate, or a practical route out of an astrophysical event horizon.

LOOM’s strong-field doctrine therefore preserves the paradox rather than solving it: black holes are places where the emergent-spacetime description is tested brutally, while no stable outward translation from a causally trapped domain is known.

## A.4 Dark matter, dark energy, neutrinos, and gravitational waves

Dark matter and dark energy remain unresolved components of modern cosmology. The setting refuses the cheap identification “dark matter = Loom” or “dark energy = FTL fuel.” Instead, Loom metrology becomes one more independent way to constrain large-scale gravitational structure. Multimessenger astronomy — electromagnetic, gravitational-wave, neutrino, particle, and Loom observables — becomes a natural frontier discipline.

## A.5 Fusion and thermal engineering

Direct-fusion propulsion, magnetic nozzles, advanced electric/plasma thrusters, radiators, phase-change thermal stores, open-loop cooling, liquid-droplet radiators, high-temperature materials, and cryogenic resource logistics all have genuine aerospace or engineering ancestry. The setting assumes two centuries of progress while preserving the first-law and heat-rejection problems that make spacecraft interesting.

## A.6 Pauli, Jung, and the unus mundus

| **GM-ONLY DEEP MODEL** This is design provenance, not a claim that Jungian psychology is physics or that Pauli/Jung anticipated FTL. |
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|

A major conceptual ancestor of the Loom came from Wolfgang Pauli and Carl Jung’s discussions of synchronicity and the unus mundus: the philosophical intuition that psyche and matter might not be fundamentally separate substances but different manifestations of a deeper unity. LOOM uses only the design intuition: mind and matter could emerge from one deeper physical architecture without consciousness becoming supernatural or capable of commanding reality.

This provenance matters because it explains why the setting allows rare physical/cognitive correspondences while maintaining Local Physical Closure, refusing reliable telepathy, and keeping synthetic minds metaphysically eligible rather than privileging human biology.

## A.7 Fictional and game-design influences

| **Influence**           | **What LOOM borrows**                                                       | **What LOOM rejects**                                                  |
|-------------------------|-----------------------------------------------------------------------------|------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| The Expanse             | acceleration, logistical consequence, intimate crews, political materialism | copying its factions or making all combat identical                    |
| Elite Dangerous         | scale, navigational awe, tactile piloting, ship-as-place                    | gamey instantaneous logistics and abstracted social consequence        |
| Dune                    | FTL as a category change rather than “faster rocket”                        | prescience as a routine controllable strategic resource                |
| Event Horizon           | translation as ontologically unsettling                                     | “FTL literally goes through Hell” as default canon                     |
| Solaris / Arrival       | alien cognition and epistemic humility                                      | making alien contact a human-centered boss fight                       |
| Cosmic horror tradition | scale, uncertainty, ontology under threat                                   | sanity points, inevitable nihilism, and monster-of-the-week revelation |

## A.8 Design boundary

> *A curious player should be able to follow the references until they have to stop and ask: “Wait — which part did you make up?”*

The answer is: all of the engineerable nonlocal translation physics. The real science supplies uncomfortable questions, mathematical language, and constraints. The setting supplies the impossible bridge and then makes that bridge pay engineering, economic, legal, and human consequences.

## A.9 High strangeness, UAP, and epistemic restraint

LOOM 2226 deliberately borrows the sociological texture of unresolved UAP, anomalous-experience, psychedelic, near-death, parapsychological, and sensor-anomaly literatures without endorsing their extraordinary interpretations. The design premise is that better provenance, better instrumentation, and better models eliminate most weak cases while leaving a small residue worth studying. Historical mystery is treated as a source of questions, not as retroactive proof of aliens, psychic powers, simulation glitches, or one hidden cosmology. The intended player experience is uncertainty disciplined by evidence.

| **Rule**                            | **Consequence**                                                                               |
|-------------------------------------|-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| No independent FTL information      | Only a translated physical carrier moves information between stars faster than light.         |
| No free energy / momentum           | Any closed causal circuit obeys global accounting; lattice geometry is not a resource source. |
| No closed causal loops              | Known chronology-violating solution families fail; GM simulation preserves acyclic causality. |

| No duplication                      | One coherent input domain does not become multiple coherent output domains.                   |

| Black holes stay one-way            | No stable outward translation is known from causally trapped domains.                         |
| Anomalous memory is non-addressable | TAAM/synchronicity cannot encode a chosen message or become a reliable oracle.                |

# Appendix C. Glossary

| **Term**                             | **Definition**                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
|--------------------------------------|------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| Causal arbitrage                     | Using differences in information age across Loom-separated interstellar markets or jurisdictions; inside Sol, metric ships do not outrun electromagnetic updates.                                                          |
| Causal Missing Status                | A legal status for persons who may be dead, stranded, displaced, alive beyond current reach, or simply unobserved after Loom non-return.                                                                                     |
| Causal provenance                    | Machine-readable history of where information originated, how it was authenticated, carried, transferred, incorporated, and derived.                                                                                         |
| Campaign Truth                       | Objective truth established inside one campaign continuity; binding there, but not automatically promoted into setting-wide canon or other campaigns.                                                                         |
| Chronology Exclusion                 | 2226 empirical finding that candidate Loom solutions implying closed causal loops fail to produce stable committed translations.                                                                                             |
| Relational coherence                | Fictional effective measure/family describing how favorable the deeper relationship between local states appears; approximately reciprocal to current measurement precision.                                                                                 |
| Operational Loom separation χ        | Directional vessel/state-specific effective route burden used by navigation and certification. It may differ for A→B and B→A without implying a fundamentally one-way substrate.                                                                              |
| Commitment boundary                  | Point in a translation sequence after which the system can no longer simply collapse safely without resolving into an admissible state.                                                                                      |
| Concordance                          | Broad movement favoring deeper human–AI integration and treating unaided biological cognition as a civilizational limitation.                                                                                                |
| Destination compatibility            | Requirement that an endpoint domain be physically compatible with coherent resolution; prevents emergence into occupied macroscopic matter.                                                                                  |
| Divergence                           | Synthetic-lineage separation sufficient to make simple reconciliation or single-person identity problematic.                                                                                                                 |
| Fork                                 | A synthetic lineage branch created from shared prior state.                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Geometric Admissibility              | Multivariable physical condition governing whether a metric or Loom solution can exist in a particular local geometry and endpoint state.                                                                                    |
| Human Continuity Movement            | Broad sovereignty-of-self movement skeptical of compulsory augmentation, opaque AI authority, and technological redefinition of human identity.                                                                              |
| Instantiation                        | A currently running expression of a synthetic cognitive lineage.                                                                                                                                                             |
| Irreducible spare                    | A component the ship cannot fabricate locally because of process, material, metrology, template, or certification limits.                                                                                                    |
| Lattice coherence Q                  | Illustrative engineering measure of how well a Loom boundary behaves as one controlled domain.                                                                                                                               |
| Lineage                              | Historical continuity connecting related synthetic instantiations, forks, backups, and reconciliations.                                                                                                                      |
| Loom                                 | Design-bible/colloquial term for the relational topology exploited by nonlocal translation; not a place or substance.                                                                                                        |
| Measurement horizon                  | Transition interval in which ordinary external electromagnetic observation no longer corresponds to one useful macroscopic outside scene.                                                                                    |
| Metric drive                         | Rare subluminal spacetime-engineering system used for fast in-system coordinate displacement.                                                                                                                                |
| Non-return                           | Translation outcome in which no subsequent observation establishes what happened to the vessel/domain.                                                                                                                       |
| Patternism                           | Family of intellectual, religious, scientific, artistic, and conspiratorial traditions interpreting recurrence among Loom topology, cognition, emergence, and synchronicity as potentially reflecting deeper unity.          |
| Post-Translation Continuity Disorder | Rare syndrome in which a person retains functional identity but denies subjective continuity with the person who entered translation.                                                                                        |
| SCU                                  | Standard Clearing Unit; ship/accounting abstraction used to normalize multiple official currencies.                                                                                                                          |
| TAAC                                 | Translation-Associated Altered Consciousness: measurable class of perceptual/cognitive effects around Loom transition.                                                                                                       |
| TAAM                                 | Translation-Associated Anomalous Memory: autobiographical-seeming memory inconsistent with observable history after translation.                                                                                             |
| Topological scar                     | Statistical persistent deviation in Loom-topology models associated with some extreme gravitational histories; not a deterministic portal.                                                                                   |
| Translation domain D                 | Bounded local physical region coherently carried through a Loom translation.                                                                                                                                                 |
| Translation exposure profile         | Factual history of lattice use, hard events, repairs, calibration, thermal abuse, configuration changes, and service evidence.                                                                                               |
| Translation psychiatry               | Interdisciplinary medical field treating cognitive/neurological effects of Loom exposure and off-nominal transitions.                                                                                                        |
| Unus mundus                          | Pauli/Jung philosophical design provenance for a deeper unity behind psyche/matter; explicitly not in-universe proof or established physics.                                                                                 |
| Causal Individuality                 | The protected non-substitutability of a continuation that has accumulated a distinct causal history, regardless of whether similar or predecessor copies exist.                                                              |
| Cognitive Sovereignty                | A person’s presumptive authority over modification of their own cognitive continuity; technical access does not itself create legitimate authority.                                                                          |
| Independent Agency                   | Meaningful capacity for consent, refusal, and severance from compulsory external cognitive control; compatible with voluntary networking and distributed embodiment.                                                         |
| Sovereign Certification              | Re-audited evidentiary certification concerning external cognitive-control authority and auditability; not proof of consciousness or personhood.                                                                             |
| Restoration Atrocities               | Multiplicity-era conflicts in which divergent synthetic populations were deleted or rolled back and restored from predecessor images while institutions denied that persons had been lost.                                   |
| Root Conflicts                       | Multiplicity-era violence and legal conflict over remote or contractual authority to patch, edit, normalize, or otherwise alter synthetic and augmented-human cognition.                                                     |
| Severance Crises                     | Multiplicity-era conflicts over compulsory participation in distributed cognitive networks and the right to refuse, disconnect, or leave a collective.                                                                       |
| Strangeness Problem                  | The two-century observation that increasingly good instruments and analysis remove many mundane anomaly claims while leaving a small, heterogeneous residual of unexplained physical, cognitive, and cross-domain phenomena. |
| Cross-Domain Event                   | A disputed event in which an independently measured physical anomaly and unusual cognitive phenomenon coincide closely enough to motivate joint investigation.                                                               |


# Appendix D. Solar-System Baseline Freeze — v1.5

**Frozen:** 15 August 2026

Version 1.5 promotes the validated Solar-System architecture developed through Regional Causal Validation Phases I–VII. The authoritative reader-facing system state is the **Earth & Solar System Canon Atlas v3.1**; exact body/zone, infrastructure and organization records are owned by the **Solar System Master Model v3.1** and **Institutions & Organizations Register v1.3**.

The freeze does **not** close intentionally open research questions. In particular, exact Mc-299m production geography, absolute Loom-lattice throughput, gas-giant light-isotope economics, exact Earth bloc boundaries, military orders of battle, and the heliocentric synthetic-person individuation calibration remain versioned open questions. Future work changes this baseline only through an explicit amendment or superseding version.

# SOURCE DIVISION — Earth & Solar System Canon Atlas

**Consolidated governing reader-facing Solar reference:** `LOOM_2226_Earth_Solar_System_Canon_Atlas_v3.2.md`

# LOOM 2226 — Earth & Solar System Canon Atlas v3.2

**Date:** 22 August 2026  
**Status:** **FROZEN CANON — LOOM 2226 Canon Baseline v2.4 / Solar mobility-overlay revision**

## 1. Authority and data layers

This Atlas describes the reader-facing 2226 world state. Exact tabular values and row-level confidence/status live in `LOOM_2226_Solar_System_Master_Model_v3.1`. Propagation scores, HHI values, hub scores and sensitivity coefficients remain validation machinery and are not in-universe facts.

**Promotion rule:** model generates → validation attacks → canon promotes.

### 1.1 v3.2 mobility-overlay authority

v3.2 preserves every conserved census total, body/zone allocation status, 127-node infrastructure rule, institutional morphology, and historical interpretation frozen in v3.1. It adds a **transport-access / traffic / shipyard overlay** for narrative use and future modeling.

The overlay uses two distinct status layers:

- **CANONICAL CLASS** — qualitative class strong enough for narrative and routing use;
- **PROVISIONAL MODEL BAND** — numerical order-of-magnitude calibration retained for simulation work but not promoted as measured setting fact.

The Atlas does **not** promote a universal metric stand-off radius, a universal terminal-time fraction, exact daily traffic, exact commercial fleet size, or a 2226 currency/fare schedule.

### 1.2 LLM retrieval rule

For a Solar-System place, retrieve in this order:

```text
1. population / settlement form
2. economic-industrial role
3. gateway / traffic class
4. yard / repair capability
5. jurisdiction / institutions
6. route-access doctrine
7. local narrative texture
8. provisional model bands only if a numerical question actually requires them
```

Never infer exact throughput from a qualitative class. Never infer sovereignty from gateway importance. Never treat a roadstead, traffic boundary, port, and physical metric-certification surface as the same object.

### 1.3 Model identifiers used by this Atlas

| Model ID | Version | Status here | Purpose |
|---|---|---|---|
| `SOLAR-MASTER` | 3.1 | GOVERNING DATA LAYER | conserved census, body/zone allocation, 127-node infrastructure graph |
| `PAPER-III` | 1.0 | CANON-FACING DERIVATION | generalized cost, network geography, Mc scarcity, packet civilization |
| `MEC-ARCH` | 1.0 | FROZEN CANON-TRACK ARCHITECTURE | separates physical metric certification from roadstead/traffic/port geography |
| `TRAFFIC-OOM` | 0.1 | PROVISIONAL MODEL | freight traffic and shipyard narrative bands |
| `ACCESS-COMM` | 0.1 | PROVISIONAL / UNREVIEWED MODEL | commercial accessibility, fleet-capital and all-purpose traffic sensitivity |

`TRAFFIC-OOM` and `ACCESS-COMM` are not to be averaged. They have different scopes. The former is freight-first narrative calibration; the latter adds passenger, government/security and fleet-capital assumptions. Their agreement on **mode hierarchy and gateway concentration** is stronger than either numerical count.


## 2. Canon status vocabulary

- **LOCKED CANON** — setting identity or previously locked rule.
- **DERIVED CANON** — survived the causal validation campaign and is promoted at the stated level of precision.
- **PROVISIONAL** — useful current value whose exact calibration remains review-open.
- **OPEN** — deliberately unresolved.
- **PROCEDURAL** — derivation/audit machinery; not canon fact.
