Orbital population share is not treated as a pure gravity proxy: family residence, work, logistics, transit and historical infrastructure are distinguished in the census registry.

## 4. System architecture in one page

- **Earth** remains the demographic, cultural, financial and institutional center and remains politically plural.
- **Cislunar space** is the densest off-Earth concentration of high-end fabrication, shipyards, finance, metrology and certification.
- **Mars** is the second broad advanced-industrial civilizational pole and can sustain ordinary civilization without continuous Earth resupply.
- **Mercury** is a small but disproportionately important extreme-process/metrology system, not an exposed-core mine or energy monoculture.
- **Venus** is a cloud/orbital civilization whose strongest demographic advantage is near-Earth gravity; water/hydrogen is its material vulnerability.
- **The Belt** is polycentric: Ceres is network/logistics/certification, Vesta is foundry/fabrication, Psyche is strategic feedstock/purification/assay.
- **Jupiter** is Callisto-led in gateway/logistics terms but politically polycentric; Ganymede is an autonomous second civic/industrial pole.
- **Saturn** is Titan-led economically/civically but not Titan-ruled; free-space habitats carry a disproportionate family role.
- **Uranus** is organized around a free-space system hub with several autonomous moon anchors.
- **Neptune** is Triton-led economically/civically while most biological residence is free-space.
- **Kuiper** is a distributed network with Pluto–Charon as gateway/processing hub, not a Pluto state.
- **Oort** is strategic science/metrology infrastructure, not territorial civilization.



## 4A. Solar mobility, gateway and shipyard overlay — v3.2

### 4A.1 Governing transport hierarchy

The mature Solar System is not a single-speed economy.

```text
TORCH   = mass, ordinary migration, bulk physical economy, local-state work
METRIC  = urgency, high-value people/cargo, rapid response, premium Solar trunk
LOOM    = sparse interstellar translation, packets, frontier access
```

Equivalent narrative shorthand:

> **Torch moves mass. Metric moves time. Loom moves the frontier.**

This is a structural economic rule, not a claim that every cargo or passenger obeys a rigid category.

Metric is a **long-baseline trunk technology**. Earth–Moon and Mars–Phobos cannot fit a complete NORMAL acquire→plateau→collapse cycle from the clean-ramp geometry alone. Other short routes may be constrained by their local environmental certification geometry. Metric is therefore not an all-purpose moon shuttle or docking system.

### 4A.2 Geography objects that must remain separate

```text
PORT
    physical loading / habitation / service infrastructure

ROADSTEAD / STAGING AREA
    operational holding, transfer, service, pilotage, traffic integration

TRAFFIC-CONTROL BOUNDARY
    administrative / safety / sequencing jurisdiction

PHYSICAL METRIC CERTIFICATION VOLUME
    environment-dependent region in which a ship/mode/ramp is or is not certifiable

PUBLISHED METRIC CORRIDOR / GATE
    operationally concentrated traffic path; NOT a literal hole in space
```

A body may have a busy port inside one orbit, a roadstead elsewhere, a traffic authority with a larger control volume, and an anisotropic metric-certification surface that changes with body geometry and ship state.

### 4A.3 Canonical qualitative classes

The following classes are promoted for **narrative scale and routing**. They are intentionally coarse.

| Region / system | Bulk torch traffic | Metric traffic | Loom access | Shipbuilding | Heavy repair | Metric overhaul | Local feeder density | Primary 2226 transport role |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Earth–Luna / cislunar | EXTREME | PRIMARY | PRIMARY GATE | PRIMARY | YES | FULL | EXTREME | financial, high-end fabrication, certification, passenger/packet and strategic gateway |
| Mars system | HIGH | MAJOR | REGIONAL GATE | MAJOR | YES | FULL | HIGH | second advanced-industrial pole; regional passenger/freight and rapid-response hub |
| Ceres + Belt network | EXTREME | MAJOR | PRIMARY GATE | PRIMARY | YES | LIMITED | EXTREME | bulk materials, remass, commercial hulls, heavy repair, Belt network exchange |
| Mercury + sunward industry | HIGH | REGIONAL | INDIRECT | REGIONAL | YES | FULL | HIGH | extreme-process materials, metric metrology, selected relational high-end fabrication |
| Venus | MODERATE | REGIONAL | INDIRECT | REGIONAL | YES | LIMITED | HIGH | cloud/orbital civilization, chemistry/biotech, inner-system trade |
| Jupiter system | HIGH | REGIONAL | INDIRECT | REGIONAL | YES | LIMITED | HIGH | Callisto gateway, Ganymede industry, major regional logistics/repair |
| Saturn system | HIGH | REGIONAL | INDIRECT | REGIONAL | YES | LIMITED | HIGH | Titan-led regional economy, chemistry, intermoon trade and repair |
| Uranus system | MODERATE | LOW | INDIRECT | REGIONAL | YES | LIMITED | MODERATE | free-space-centered ice-giant hub and autonomous moon network |
| Neptune system | MODERATE | LOW | INDIRECT | REGIONAL | YES | LIMITED | MODERATE | Triton/free-space regional network, cryogenic and outer-system logistics |
| Kuiper / scattered / detached | MODERATE | LOW | INDIRECT | LOCAL / REGIONAL | YES | LIMITED | MODERATE | Pluto–Charon gateway plus distributed charters and processing nodes |
| Oort | LOW / RESTRICTED | LOW | NONE | LOCAL | NO | NO | LOW | science, metrology, relay and strategic observation |
| Distributed heliocentric network | MODERATE | REGIONAL | INDIRECT | REGIONAL | SELECTED | LIMITED | MODERATE | process, relay, metric metrology, science and specialized fabrication |

**Class interpretation:** `PRIMARY` does not mean monopoly. `INDIRECT` Loom access means routine connection through another gateway rather than a major local formation complex. `LIMITED` metric overhaul means field-array service and certification support may exist but the most demanding rebuild/requalification work is concentrated elsewhere.

### 4A.4 Narrative traffic texture by region

**Earth–Luna.** The inner orbital network feels like a mature global aviation-and-container system translated into three-dimensional space: continuous feeder traffic, tugs, workboats, tankers and torch departures, with metric movements ordinary enough to be scheduled infrastructure rather than spectacle. A Loom movement is security-heavy and institutionally visible even when the hardware is familiar.

**Mars.** Long-haul arrivals are routine and the orbital system is busy, but less saturated than cislunar space. Metric ships are common enough that nobody stops work to stare; slots, certification state, legal priority and terminal access still matter. Phobos/Deimos are transfer geography, not magical resource rocks.

**Ceres/Belt.** The Belt is the place where low population and huge material traffic coexist. Ceres traffic control, service and insurance can be intensely busy because the network moves water, remass, structural feedstock and ships. Heavy commercial ship work is ordinary. Precision relational overhaul remains more dependent on inner-system specialist capacity than bulk yard work.

**Mercury / heliocentric.** The traffic is smaller in human terms and disproportionately technical: metrology craft, high-end materials, sunward industrial movements and specialized metric work. A quiet station can be strategically important despite modest passenger volume.

**Venus.** Most traffic is orbital/cloud-linked. Local shuttle and feeder movements are dense relative to interplanetary calls because surface access is not the settlement norm.

**Jupiter.** Callisto absorbs much of the interplanetary arrival culture: yards, remass, rescue, convoy and transfer. Ganymede retains its own industrial/civic traffic. The system is busy enough for regional scheduling, but a metric movement remains more noticeable than at Earth or Mars.

**Saturn.** Titan sets much of the economic tempo, while intermoon logistics create dense local motion. Metric is a specialized rapid layer over a much larger torch/feeder network.

**Uranus / Neptune.** Regional hubs are mature civilizations, not lonely stations, but metric arrivals are still events with schedule and institutional visibility. Local ice-giant networks depend heavily on intra-system feeders and repair capacity.

**Kuiper.** Traffic is distributed and schedule-sensitive. Pluto–Charon is a major gateway because network effects accumulate there, not because it rules the region.

**Oort.** The density is low enough that a ship arrival can materially change local social and operational state. Metrology and causal information matter more than commodity throughput.

### 4A.5 Provisional numerical calibration — NOT canon fact

Use only when an order-of-magnitude calculation is needed.

`TRAFFIC-OOM v0.1`, freight-first central case:

```text
inter-system torch freight       ~3 Bt/y central; ~1–6 Bt/y sensitivity
loaded long-haul torch legs      ~2,000/day
freight arrivals+departures      ~4,000/day Solar-wide
metric physical freight          ~20 Mt/y central; ~5–50 Mt/y sensitivity
freight-oriented metric calls    ~200–300/day Solar-wide
local feeder/tug/mining motion   ~4–8× long-haul cargo movement count
Loom transits                    single digits to a few tens/day (starter band only)
```

`ACCESS-COMM v0.1`, broader commercial sensitivity case:

```text
long-haul torch commercial ships physically in flight   ~95,000 central-study result
installed relational hulls                               ~530 central-study result
intra-Solar metric-capable ships in flight               ~174
ships actually in continuous metric trunk                 ~44
all-purpose metric horizon events                         ~790/day
```

These values are **PROVISIONAL MODEL OUTPUTS**. `ACCESS-COMM` uses new assumptions about passenger demand, commercial ship designs, Mc allocation, value of time and utilization and has not yet completed independent hostile review. It may inform scenes and sensitivity work; it may not silently harden into exact canon.

### 4A.6 Traffic-model fidelity rule

Default runtime fidelity:

```text
Earth–Luna       COARSE HOURLY CAPACITY / QUEUE STATE when relevant
Mars             LIGHT HOURLY/DAILY CAPACITY MODEL when relevant
Ceres/Belt       DAILY BAND + NARRATIVE SEQUENCING by default
Other systems    NARRATIVE + CLASS unless plot/economics requires more
```

Do **not** instantiate every Solar metric ship merely to create background traffic. Generate individual vessels only when they can affect the player, a queue, an investigation, a contract, a rescue problem, or a strategically relevant flow.

Torch traffic is different: its aggregate population is large enough that the GM should treat deep-space commercial motion as persistent background infrastructure even when individual hulls are not instantiated.

### 4A.7 Future route-flow schema

If a later campaign needs quantitative transport assignment, use this minimum record per hub/corridor:

```text
HUB_ID
SYSTEM
TRAFFIC_CLASS_TORCH
TRAFFIC_CLASS_METRIC
LOOM_ACCESS_CLASS
SHIPYARD_CLASS
HEAVY_REPAIR
METRIC_OVERHAUL
LOCAL_FEEDER_CLASS
CERTIFICATION_STANDARD_ID
CERT_COEFFICIENT_SET_ID
TRAFFIC_CONTROL_AUTHORITY
ROADSTEAD_ID / STATUS
PHYSICAL_CERT_MODEL_ID
OD_MODEL_ID
CAPACITY_MODEL_ID
MODEL_STATUS
VALID_FROM / VALID_UNTIL
PROVENANCE
```

The future model chain is:

```text
conserved demographics / industry
        ↓
origin-destination demand
        ↓
mode choice / generalized cost
        ↓
fleet & Mc-capital constraint
        ↓
route assignment
        ↓
port / roadstead / corridor capacity
        ↓
queueing, disruption and accessibility
```

Do not reverse this chain by using desired traffic to choose metric-physics coefficients.


## 5. Earth, Earth orbit and cislunar civilization

Earth remains a plural terrestrial civilization rather than a United Earth. Earth orbit contains ~64.74M permanent biological residents in dense habitats, industrial zones, finance and metrology networks. Lunar/cislunar civilization contains ~14.96M biological residents; most long-term family growth occurs in rotating orbital/cislunar habitats rather than relying on one-sixth gravity as biologically solved.

## 6. Mars, Phobos and Deimos

Mars contains ~60.29M biological residents across large surface regions, orbital habitats/shipyards and the Phobos/Deimos transfer systems. It is a substantial independent civilization with local water/atmospheric chemistry, agriculture, construction, machine industry and high-end yards. Phobos and Deimos matter because of orbital geography, not assumed magical ores.

## 7. Mercury and heliocentric industry

Mercury contains ~9.24M biological residents and ~6.53M recognized synthetic persons. Its strategic niche combines polar water, unusual reduced chemistry, extreme process environments, precision/metrology infrastructure and sunward geometry. High solar flux helps but does not by itself explain the civilization. Distributed heliocentric stations remain non-territorial process, science, relay and metrology infrastructure.

## 8. Venus

Venus contains ~26.28M biological residents: overwhelmingly aerostat/cloud and orbital. The surface is almost entirely robotic/scientific. Near-Earth gravity in the cloud layer is the major settlement advantage; atmospheric chemistry and biotech support the civilization. Hydrogen/water inventory and replacement losses remain the primary material vulnerability.

## 9. Ceres and the Belt

Ceres remains the strongest Belt civic/network hub because water/reaction mass, ship service, finance, insurance, standards and traffic reinforce one another. It does not control the Belt.

**Vesta** is the differentiated foundry/fabrication anchor. **Psyche** is the strategic-feedstock, separation, purification and assay cluster. **Pallas** remains science/specialty extraction. **Hygiea** remains a secondary volatile supplier. Smaller bodies range from niche automated works to reserves, preserves and failed/non-promoted sites.

### Vesta

Vesta is the Belt’s dry foundry world: differentiated material and long industrial history matter more than population. Its orbital habitats make the industrial node livable without pretending Vesta’s native gravity is family-normal.

**2226 biological residents:** ~1.14M  
**Recognized synthetic persons:** ~768.7k *(role-weighted within conserved system total unless explicitly derived)*
**Role:** Differentiated Belt foundry/fabrication anchor  
**Resources/environment:** Basaltic/silicate/metal/refractory feedstock  
**Industry:** Ceramics, structural materials, refractory intermediates and qualified fabrication  
**Settlement constraints:** Extremely low native gravity; ordinary family life primarily orbital  
**Civil/institutional form:** Vesta Industrial Authority + Belt network institutions  
**Security:** Belt Security & Rescue Directorate  
**Major node IDs:** VST-P01; VST-P02  

**Regional relationship:** Complementary to Psyche feedstock and Ceres network power.

### Psyche

Psyche is valuable because its feedstock can be characterized, purified and trusted—not because it is a treasure chest of pure metal. Its industrial culture is correspondingly heavy on assay, provenance and anti-capture governance.

**2226 biological residents:** ~240.0k  
**Recognized synthetic persons:** ~720.0k *(role-weighted within conserved system total unless explicitly derived)*
**Role:** Systemically important strategic-feedstock/purification/assay cluster  
**Resources/environment:** Metal/rock mixture with strong strategic-feedstock potential; no “solid metal asteroid” assumption  
**Industry:** Extraction, beneficiation, primary refining, specialty separation, substantial ultra-purification, feedstock assay/metrology  
**Settlement constraints:** Tiny native gravity; most biological family residence is rotating/free-space  
**Civil/institutional form:** Psyche Industrial Commonwealth; Belt Transit Authority; Psyche Materials Assurance Authority  
**Security:** Belt Security & Rescue Directorate  
**Major node IDs:** PSY-P01; PSY-P02; PSY-P03  

**Regional relationship:** Psyche does not receive automatic Mc synthesis, Loom fabrication or final public certification.


## 10. Jupiter system

The Jupiter system contains ~24.18M biological residents. Callisto is the principal interplanetary gateway; Ganymede is the autonomous second civic/industrial pole; Europa is protected science/quarantine; Io is hazardous specialist infrastructure. Civil jurisdiction remains local. Jovian navigation, rescue and convoy safety are coordinated through the **Jovian Navigation & Rescue Compact**, while technical reference/recognition is coordinated through the **Jovian Metrology Forum** rather than a generic three-agency system stack.

### Callisto

Callisto feels like a frontier port that accidentally became indispensable: less glamorous than inner Galilean science, but safer, easier to reach and saturated with the habits of crews, yards, insurers and rescue services. Its political weight comes from people repeatedly needing Callisto—not from a constitutional claim to Jupiter.

**2226 biological residents:** ~12.56M  
**Recognized synthetic persons:** ~4.61M *(role-weighted within conserved system total unless explicitly derived)*
**Role:** Principal Jovian gateway/logistics/ship-service anchor  
**Resources/environment:** Water ice, rock, oxygen-bearing feedstock; lower radiation burden than inner Galileans  
**Industry:** Reaction mass, ship service, logistics, local industry  
**Settlement constraints:** 0.126g surface; family life strongly supported by orbital artificial-g habitats  
**Civil/institutional form:** Callisto Commonwealth; Jovian Navigation & Rescue Compact  
**Security:** Jovian Navigation & Rescue Compact  
**Major node IDs:** JOV-P01; JOV-P02; JOV-P03  

**Jovian position:** Callisto is the first stop for much system traffic, not the political owner of the other Galileans.

### Ganymede

Ganymede is the Jovian system’s large civic counterweight to Callisto: more radiation burden, more shielding and infrastructure cost, but enough population, industry and institutional depth to sustain a distinct public life. Its surface districts and orbital ring are one political society with very different daily environments.

**2226 biological residents:** ~7.87M  
**Recognized synthetic persons:** ~3.53M *(role-weighted within conserved system total unless explicitly derived)*
**Role:** Autonomous second Jovian civic and industrial center  
**Resources/environment:** Water ice, silicate rock, oxygen-bearing materials; high science value; radiation-managed  
**Industry:** Ice/rock processing, Jovian industrial supply, science, logistics, orbital industry  
**Settlement constraints:** 0.146g and Jovian radiation; shielded surface districts plus large artificial-g orbital ring  
**Civil/institutional form:** Ganymede Civic Authority; shared Jovian transit/standards institutions  
**Security:** Jovian Navigation & Rescue Compact  
**Major node IDs:** JOV-P04; JOV-P05  

**Jovian position:** Ganymede trades influence with Callisto rather than taking orders from it; Callisto wins the gateway, Ganymede retains civic/industrial autonomy.

### Europa

Europa is deliberately inconvenient. Access procedures, quarantine and evidentiary discipline are designed to make exploitation slower than curiosity, because the ocean’s scientific value is harder to replace than Jovian water.

**2226 biological residents:** ~1.89M  
**Recognized synthetic persons:** ~884.1k *(role-weighted within conserved system total unless explicitly derived)*
**Role:** Protected ocean-science/quarantine world  
**Resources/environment:** Water ice/ocean science, salts/sulfur compounds; high astrobiological value  
**Industry:** Controlled science/sample handling; ordinary commodity extraction suppressed  
**Settlement constraints:** High radiation and contamination risk; sparse protected surface presence, large orbital science ring  
**Civil/institutional form:** Europa Ocean Protection Authority  
**Security:** Jovian Navigation & Rescue Compact  
**Major node IDs:** JOV-P06; JOV-P07  

**Regional relationship:** Scientific uniqueness outweighs generic water value.

### Io

Io is a place civilization mostly operates **through machines**. Human and synthetic specialist presence exists because the environment is scientifically and industrially unique, not because anyone confused it with a normal settlement.

**2226 biological residents:** ~316.5k  
**Recognized synthetic persons:** ~225.0k *(role-weighted within conserved system total unless explicitly derived)*
**Role:** Hazardous specialist/robotic operations node  
**Resources/environment:** Sulfur/silicates, extreme volcanism and radiation  
**Industry:** Volcanology, remote operations, niche materials/science  
**Settlement constraints:** Extreme radiation/volcanism; biology sparse  
**Civil/institutional form:** Io Hazard Operations Charter  
**Security:** Jovian Navigation & Rescue Compact  
**Major node IDs:** JOV-P08  


## 11. Saturn system

Saturn contains ~31.27M biological residents, making it the largest outer-system biological civilization in the central model. Titan is the industrial/economic/civic anchor, but Saturn is **Titan-led, not Titan-ruled**. Enceladus is a protected ocean-science jurisdiction. Rhea, Dione, Tethys, Iapetus, Mimas and Phoebe retain distinct local/functional roles; free-space habitats are a major family and ship-service environment. The **Saturn Intermoon Compact** coordinates routes, commerce arbitration and mutual rescue; security remains treaty-based and locally supplied rather than a Saturnian unitary ministry.

### Titan

Titan is Saturn’s great material civilization: atmosphere, hydrocarbons, aviation and surface logistics give it an everyday physical texture unlike any other outer-system moon. Its very low gravity, however, means political/cultural Titan and family-development Titan are not always the same geography.

**2226 biological residents:** ~18.17M  
**Recognized synthetic persons:** ~5.40M *(role-weighted within conserved system total unless explicitly derived)*
**Role:** Saturnian industrial/economic/civic anchor  
**Resources/environment:** Dense N2 atmosphere, methane/ethane chemistry, water-ice bedrock, organics  
**Industry:** Chemistry, habitat industry, surface logistics/aviation, ship service  
**Settlement constraints:** 0.138g: surface adult life is viable but ordinary family development leans heavily on artificial-g free-space habitats  
**Civil/institutional form:** Titan Commonwealth; Saturn Intermoon Compact  
**Security:** Saturn Mutual Rescue & Security Treaty  
**Major node IDs:** SAT-P01; SAT-P02; SAT-P03; SAT-P04  

**Saturnian position:** Titan sets much of the economic tempo, while intermoon compacts and local jurisdictions prevent that weight from becoming automatic sovereignty.

### Enceladus

Enceladus matters precisely because the easiest material to obtain there—water—is also the least interesting reason to touch it. Its plume/ocean regime is built around contamination control and scientific trust rather than commodity throughput.

**2226 biological residents:** ~1.06M  
**Recognized synthetic persons:** ~507.9k *(role-weighted within conserved system total unless explicitly derived)*
**Role:** Protected ocean/plume science regime  
**Resources/environment:** Water plume, salts, organics, ocean access  
**Industry:** Science, sterile sample handling, remote operations  
**Settlement constraints:** 0.011g and planetary-protection constraints; surface presence tightly limited  
**Civil/institutional form:** Enceladus Ocean Protection Authority  
**Security:** Saturn Mutual Rescue & Security Treaty  
**Major node IDs:** SAT-P05; SAT-P06  

**Regional relationship:** Plume access is not treated as a free commodity-water hose.

### Phoebe

Phoebe is useful as an information-producing place: samples, provenance, survey staging and irregular-moon context. Treating that knowledge role as secondary to bulk mining would make it less distinctive and less valuable.

**2226 biological residents:** ~101.1k  
**Recognized synthetic persons:** ~11.0k *(role-weighted within conserved system total unless explicitly derived)*
**Role:** Outer-Saturn survey/provenance/staging node  
**Resources/environment:** Observed water ice, CO2, ferric iron and primitive/dark material  
**Industry:** Survey, sample characterization, provenance/archive, irregular-moon staging  
**Settlement constraints:** Tiny gravity; low biological population  
**Civil/institutional form:** Phoebe Survey Charter / Saturn Outer Survey Authority  
**Security:** Saturn Mutual Rescue & Security Treaty  
**Major node IDs:** SAT-P12  

**Regional relationship:** No bulk-mine assumption.


## 12. Uranus

Uranus contains ~8.08M biological residents and is best understood as a **free-space-centered network**, not a Titania-centered miniature Saturn. The Uranus Free-Space Exchange/shipyard-habitat network is the systemwide hub. Titania is the largest moon civic anchor; Ariel is a high-value science/industry node; Oberon is an outer gateway; Umbriel is industrial; Miranda is science-led. Local civil jurisdictions remain distinct.

## 13. Neptune

Neptune contains ~6.84M biological residents. Triton remains the principal economic/civic/cryogenic anchor, while most biological residents live in artificial-g free-space environments. Proteus is inner service; Nereid is science/outer transfer and secondary reserve. Triton does not govern the entire system.

## 14. Kuiper and Oort

The Kuiper/scattered/detached network contains ~3.22M biological and ~5.44M synthetic persons. Pluto–Charon is the strongest gateway/processing complex but not a regional sovereign. Haumea, Makemake, Eris and the distributed Quaoar–Orcus–Gonggong network retain local niches and charters.

Oort contains only ~242 biological and ~1.2k recognized synthetic persons in a handful of deep science/metrology/relay stations. It has no meaningful territorial government or commodity economy.

## 15. Synthetic persons and political pluralism

Synthetic persons are citizens/residents/participants across many jurisdictions, not a single polity. Three major non-territorial political traditions are now named: **Continuity Assembly**, **Lineage Cooperative Network**, and **Autonomous Personhood League**. They disagree over continuity, lineage, cooperative governance, refusal, severance and autonomy; none represents all synthetic persons.

### 15.1 Synthetic compute geography

Compute/substrate location is politically meaningful because it can be residence, embodiment support, employment infrastructure and a continuity chokepoint. The Solar census fixes recognized synthetic-person totals where stated, but no universal embodied/mobile/data-center percentage is promoted. Exact residency and individuation geography remains open.

## 16. Economy and strategic industry

> **Money is easy. Trust, authority, settlement and access are hard.**

> **Mine/extract → separation → ultra-purification → qualified fabrication → metrology → certification.**

Raw resources are widely distributed. The highest-value industrial power concentrates progressively upward in transformation, fabrication, metrology, provenance and certification. Cislunar space is the strongest geographic concentration of high-end fabrication/proof capability, while Mars retains an independent advanced stack and Mercury carries specialist process/metrology capacity. Outer-system bulk economies remain regional.

The named major corporate lineages are **Asteria Ship Systems**, **Helios Materials Group**, **Axiom Precision & Metrology**, **Ferrum Meridian**, **Concord Mutual Infrastructure & Assurance**, and **Vitae Habitat & Biotechnics**. Helios concentrates on ultra-purity chemistry, specialty separation and advanced material transformation; Ferrum is the throughput-heavy primary-metallurgy/feedstock lineage, so the two frequently sit on opposite sides of the same qualification chain rather than being duplicate logos. The first five behave as system-scale megacorporate ecosystems; Vitae is a major habitat/biotech sectoral sixth. Corporate leverage comes from dependencies, capital and infrastructure—not automatic civil sovereignty.

### 16.1 Merchant reserve and wartime logistics

Commercial torch shipping is the strategic sealift base. Reserve charters, reaction-mass tankers, modular auxiliary conversions, casualty evacuation, repair/support ships and war-risk insurance are established institutions. Exact reserve fractions, auxiliary counts and military allocations remain model/open questions rather than Atlas facts. Requisition of a vessel does not itself transfer cognitive ownership of recognized synthetic crew.

## 17. Power and governance doctrine

Bodies are not actors. Ports are not governments. Population, economic activity, industrial throughput, strategic importance and military/security leverage are different maps.

**Civil jurisdiction is local; network power propagates by function.** Transit, rescue, standards, metrology, survey and security institutions may legitimately operate systemwide. Gateway leadership or commercial leadership alone does not grant sovereignty.

### 17.1 Law, security and coercive infrastructure

There is no single Solar police authority. Within Sol, warrants and alerts can propagate at light speed even though recognition and enforcement remain local; a metric ship does not outrun the broadcast. Deep-space registry/charter authority, local port/gateway law, treaty security powers, standards/certification and insurer access therefore overlap without collapsing into one sovereign hierarchy. Metric interdiction concentrates at endpoints, roadsteads and support/certification infrastructure rather than assuming routine mid-course capture.

For off-Earth biological communities, atmosphere, pressure, heat, water and radiation protection are engineered infrastructure. Control of those systems can become extreme coercive leverage, but legal safeguards, ownership models and emergency powers vary. The Atlas does not imply that all operators may lawfully weaponize life support.

Across Loom links, synchronized legal and administrative state must physically ride the packet network; this is where genuine information-age arbitrage and causally stale warrants arise.

## 18. Major infrastructure graph

The authoritative routing graph contains **127 major/strategic nodes**. Low-traffic science/protection/metrology nodes remain major when consequence-if-lost or protected scientific function—not passenger traffic—is the reason for inclusion. The three Psyche nodes are the only additions to the former 124-node v2.0.1 graph. Minor mines, municipal pads, repair docks and work sites remain abstracted below this fidelity.

## 19. Chronology

The mature Solar-System settlement map is overwhelmingly a torch-era creation. Metric compresses urgency/high-value mobility and strengthens strategic institutions; it does not erase bulk geography. Loom's operational period is only a few years by 2226 and cannot explain mature pre-existing regional governments or populations.

## 20. Known open questions after v3.2 mobility update

The following remain intentionally unresolved or provisional and do not invalidate the baseline:

- Heliocentric recognized-synthetic population: **7.50M remains a provisional system-level personhood calibration** pending dedicated individuation/staffing closure.
- Exact Mc-299m production-site shares and industrial pathway.
- Absolute Loom-lattice throughput and deeper coherence/constitutive scaling.
- Gas-giant ³He/light-isotope economics.
- Exact terrestrial bloc borders and detailed military orders of battle.
- Exact wartime auxiliary reserve fraction, relational-hull role allocation and government/commercial split below the provisional aggregate fleet models.
- Exact synthetic-person embodiment/residency/individuation geography below conserved census totals.
- Sub-million body-level demographic refinements where the Master Model marks allocations as provisional/reconciled rather than independently cohort-derived.

- Exact commercial metric fleet registry, passenger/freight split and Mc allocation by sector.
- Exact Solar origin-destination passenger and freight matrix.
- Exact daily/seasonal gateway throughput, corridor capacity and queue distributions.
- Exact 2226 fares, wages, currency translation and value-of-time distribution.
- Exact shipyard annual build/repair capacity below the qualitative yard classes.
- Exact metric environmental certification surfaces and the calibrated continuous state propagator `U^M_gamma`.

## 21. Canon freeze ruling

As of **22 August 2026**, the v3.1 physical/demographic/industrial/institutional freeze remains intact and the v3.2 **qualitative mobility, traffic and yard classes** are promoted into **LOOM 2226 Canon Baseline v2.4**. Numerical traffic, fleet, fare, OD-demand and environmental-certification values remain explicitly model-status controlled. Later work may refine provisional values, but changes to a locked structural result require a versioned canon amendment or superseding baseline.

## 22. v3.2 freeze notes

- Recognized-synthetic body allocations are role-weighted within conserved system totals rather than flat proportional smears.
- Orbital residence is explicitly separated from family-development interpretation; orbital population is not a pure gravity proxy.
- The three named synthetic political organizations are major interregional traditions, not exhaustive factions.
- Regional network governance varies by history and scale rather than using one universal Transit/Standards/Security template.
- **Free space** means orbital/rotating habitats, yards, stations and other settlements not resident on a natural surface; it is not a polity named “Free Space.”
- Asteria retains a system-scale shipyard/service moat, while Mars preserves indigenous strategic yard capacity through the Elysium Orbital Works Consortium.
- Psyche's three major nodes are PSY-P01, PSY-P02 and PSY-P03.


- Traffic/yard classes are canonical narrative scale; exact calls/day and fleet counts are not.
- `TRAFFIC-OOM v0.1` and `ACCESS-COMM v0.1` remain auditable provisional models with different scopes.
- Physical metric certification, traffic control, roadstead and port geometry are explicitly separate.
- Earth–Moon and Mars–Phobos full NORMAL-cycle short-haul exclusions are derived from clean-ramp geometry; no universal acquisition radius is implied.
- Future traffic models must be downstream of physics and conserved demographics, never used to tune M2 coefficients.

# SOURCE DIVISION — Solar System Master Model

**Consolidated from:** `LOOM_2226_Solar_System_Master_Model_v3.1.md`



# LOOM 2226 — Solar System Master Model v3.1

**Frozen:** 15 August 2026  
**Authority:** Tier-B authoritative model for **LOOM 2226 Solar-System Canon Baseline v1.0**

## 1. Purpose and hierarchy

This file consolidates the project-source view of the body-level census, world-state registry and 127-node infrastructure/power map. It owns exact tabular world-state facts at the current model fidelity. The Atlas summarizes these values for readers; validation scores, HHI values and alternative sensitivity runs live in the Validation & Provenance Archive and are not in-universe facts.

**Authority rules:**

- Global and system totals are conserved authorities.
- Body/zone values carry explicit status; reconciled allocations are not misrepresented as independently simulated cohort outputs.
- Recognized synthetic persons are persons, not automation/capital.
- The heliocentric 7.50M synthetic-person total is **PROVISIONAL_SYSTEM_PERSONHOOD_CALIBRATION_REQUIRES_INDIVIDUATION_AUDIT**.
- Orbital/free-space residence may be family-primary, work/science-primary or mixed; orbital share is not a pure proxy for native gravity.
- The infrastructure graph has 127 major/strategic nodes; smaller local infrastructure remains below this fidelity.

# SOURCE DIVISION — Institutions & Organizations Register

**Consolidated from:** `LOOM_2226_Institutions_Organizations_Register_v1.3.md`

# LOOM 2226 — Institutions & Organizations Register v1.3

**Status:** **FROZEN CANON — LOOM 2226 Canon Baseline v2.3**. Existing working names are canonical unless explicitly marked otherwise; xenoarchaeology-era predecessor genealogy, subsidiary ownership and exact market shares remain open.

## Megacorporate lineages

### Asteria Ship Systems
**Role:** Shipyards, integration, fleet service
**Boundary:** Strong yard-integration/service moat; commercial presence does not imply yard ownership, civil jurisdiction, standards authority or security control. Mars contains an indigenous protected challenger.

### Helios Materials Group
**Role:** Advanced materials transformation: ultra-purity chemistry, specialty separation, ceramics/intermetallics, certified high-performance precursors
**Boundary:** Transforms qualified feedstocks into high-performance material systems; buys from and competes with bulk/refining lineages rather than duplicating them.

### Axiom Precision & Metrology
**Role:** Precision manufacturing, metrology, high-end fabrication
**Boundary:** Technically indispensable; public certification remains institutionally separate.

### Ferrum Meridian
**Role:** Primary metallurgy and heavy industrial feedstock: refractory concentrates, structural metals, bulk refining, rough qualified stock
**Boundary:** Throughput-heavy Belt/Mars industrial lineage; commonly supplies feedstock to Helios/Axiom rather than owning downstream precision transformation.

### Concord Mutual Infrastructure & Assurance
**Role:** Finance, insurance, provenance, infrastructure assurance
**Boundary:** Network/contract power rather than territorial rule.

### Vitae Habitat & Biotechnics
**Role:** Habitats, biotechnology, life-support systems
**Boundary:** Major sectoral sixth; less system-spanning than first five.

## Artifact-order corporate lineage effects

The six 2226 lineages are not “alien-tech corporations.” Their predecessor capabilities became unusually valuable during the century-long Artifact Acceleration after 1I. Exact merger trees remain open, but the broad capability inheritance is canon:

- **Asteria** — access, salvage, ship integration, expedition architecture and later artifact-response logistics.
- **Helios** — ultra-purity materials, alien structural/propulsion-material characterization and high-end transformation.
- **Axiom** — atom-scale metrology, substrate-state analysis and precision fabrication; strongly associated in public imagination with Black Nursery data.
- **Ferrum** — isotope precursor scale, primary nuclear/material feedstock and industrialization of laboratory exotica.
- **Concord** — finite-sample provenance, custody assurance, artifact insurance and evidentiary chain validation.
- **Vitae** — Nursery biology, symbiosis, developmental systems, cryobiology and biological/synthetic interface research.

Commercial capability does not confer sovereign ownership of alien material or public certification authority. Under the Oumuamua Custody Partition, physical custody, raw data, analytical control, provenance authority and exploitation rights are intentionally separable.

No lineage canonically possesses a complete Builder reconstruction, intact alien AI, complete alien drive or unique “truth” about the Great Filter.

## Synthetic-person political ecology

The Continuity Assembly, Lineage Cooperative Network and Autonomous Personhood League are the three largest **interregional organizations**, not an exhaustive ideological taxonomy. Membership, sympathy and issue coalitions overlap. Cross-cutting labor, embodiment, spiritual/patternist and professional currents operate inside and outside all three.

- **Continuity Assembly** — Legal continuity, recognition, established rights  
  Boundary: One major tradition; no synthetic state; one of three largest interregional traditions, not an exhaustive ideology or exclusive membership bloc.
- **Lineage Cooperative Network** — Lineage/fork governance, industrial cooperative politics  
  Boundary: Strong in industrial/deep-system environments; one of three largest interregional traditions, not an exhaustive ideology or exclusive membership bloc.
- **Autonomous Personhood League** — Refusal, severance, autonomy, exit rights  
  Boundary: Smaller but meaningful frontier/free-space tradition; one of three largest interregional traditions, not an exhaustive ideology or exclusive membership bloc.
- **Synthetic Labor & Co-Determination Current** — Labor governance, ownership, worker representation and industrial co-determination  
  Boundary: Cross-cuts the three major synthetic organizations; not an exclusive membership party.
- **Embodiment & Locality Current** — Politics of local embodiment, distributed instantiation, substrate access and physical dependency  
  Boundary: Cross-cutting current rather than a territorial faction.
- **Patternist & Spiritual Continuity Currents** — Religious/philosophical views of pattern, continuity, reconciliation and personhood  
  Boundary: Multiple movements; not one church or state.
- **Professional & Scientific Synthetic Networks** — Professional/scientific interests, institutional autonomy and research ethics  
  Boundary: Often overlaps Continuity, Lineage or autonomy politics.

## Xenoarchaeology-era human political ecology

These are movement families and issue coalitions, not sovereign states and not exhaustive ideologies.

- **Human Continuity / Continuity Naturalist current** — baseline-population preservation, cognitive/genetic sovereignty, precaution around irreversible developmental modification, opposition to compulsory augmentation. Moderate forms are mainstream civil-libertarian/precautionary politics; exclusionary and violent variants exist.
- **Expansionist current** — supports rapid multi-system settlement and redundant industrial/population continuity; iconic slogan **Distance is Insurance**.
- **The Convergence** — loose historical/political label for tactical cooperation among otherwise incompatible anti-system currents around “Stop the Irreversible Transition.” No central leadership or unified platform; a broken-ring symbol is common. Includes lawful activism, leak/sabotage networks and a much smaller lethal rollback fringe.
- **Artifact Transparency / Release currents** — demand public access to alien evidence on the grounds that species-level evidence cannot legitimately remain proprietary or classified; synthetic-rights variants claim direct standing over Black Nursery evidence.
- **Stay / anti-panic current** — argues that terminal mobilization may itself be maladaptive and opposes treating weak TMP statistics as justification for civilization-wide dispersal.

These currents cross-cut existing terrestrial blocs, Mars/Belt politics, synthetic-person organizations, religions, corporations and frontier settlements.

## Regional/public institutions

Phase-VII Step-11 removes the prior one-template Transit/Standards/Security stack. Mature systems can still support specialized agencies, but sparse or historically different regions bundle functions into compacts, exchanges, treaty networks or mission authorities.

- **Earth Orbital Traffic Compact** (PUBLIC_NETWORK; Earth Orbit) — Orbital traffic/interoperability. Functional authority; does not replace Earth states.
- **Lunar Transit & Standards Authority** (PUBLIC_NETWORK; Moon/cislunar) — Transit and common operating standards. Coexists with local lunar/cislunar polities.
- **Cislunar Standards Directorate** (PUBLIC_STANDARDS; Moon/cislunar) — Metric/high-end standards and certification. Public proof layer separate from manufacturers.
- **Earth–Luna Strategic Coordination Command** (SECURITY_COALITION; Earth–Moon) — Strategic security/coordination. Security authority, not civil government.
- **Mars Orbital Transit Authority** (PUBLIC_NETWORK; Mars) — Orbital traffic/transfer infrastructure. Systemwide functional scope.
- **Mars Metric Standards Directorate** (PUBLIC_STANDARDS; Mars) — Metric/metrology standards. Independent of commercial fabricators.
- **Mars System Defence & Rescue** (SECURITY_COALITION; Mars) — Defence, rescue, convoy protection. Order of battle remains open.
- **Belt Transit Authority** (PUBLIC_NETWORK; Ceres/Belt/Psyche) — Belt traffic and transfer standards. Network authority; local bodies retain civil government.
- **Belt Standards Directorate** (PUBLIC_STANDARDS; Ceres/Belt) — Materials/industrial standards. Ceres-centered network function.
- **Belt Security & Rescue Directorate** (SECURITY_COALITION; Belt) — Rescue/security. No territorial Belt state implied.
- **Deep System Metrology Authority** (PUBLIC_STANDARDS; Kuiper/Oort) — Deep-system metrology/route standards. Mission/network authority, not territorial state.
- **Deep System Survey Authority** (SCIENCE_NETWORK; Kuiper/Oort) — Survey/navigation/relay. Mission network.
- **Distributed Solar Infrastructure Compact** (PUBLIC_NETWORK; Heliocentric) — Non-territorial infrastructure interoperability. Solar infrastructure compact, not state.
- **Inner Solar Security Directorate** (SECURITY_COALITION; Mercury/heliocentric) — Inner-Solar security. Functional security scope.
- **Ceres Commonwealth** (REGIONAL_GOVERNMENT; Ceres) — Ceres civil government. Ceres network leadership does not equal Belt sovereignty.
- **Psyche Industrial Commonwealth** (REGIONAL_GOVERNMENT; Psyche) — Psyche civil government. Post-Multiplicity anti-capture industrial jurisdiction.
- **Callisto Commonwealth** (REGIONAL_GOVERNMENT; Callisto) — Callisto civil government. Principal Jovian gateway; not Jovian government.
- **Ganymede Civic Authority** (REGIONAL_GOVERNMENT; Ganymede) — Ganymede civil government. Autonomous second Jovian civic/industrial pole.
- **Europa Ocean Protection Authority** (SCIENCE_REGULATOR; Europa) — Planetary protection / civil access regime. Independent protection jurisdiction.
- **Titan Commonwealth** (REGIONAL_GOVERNMENT; Titan) — Titan civil government. Saturn anchor; not Saturn sovereign.
- **Enceladus Ocean Protection Authority** (SCIENCE_REGULATOR; Enceladus) — Planetary protection / sterile access. Independent protection jurisdiction.
- **Titania Commonwealth** (REGIONAL_GOVERNMENT; Titania) — Titania civil government. Largest Uranian moon polity; free-space is system hub.
- **Triton Commonwealth** (REGIONAL_GOVERNMENT; Triton) — Triton civil government. Neptune civic/industrial anchor; not system sovereign.
- **Pluto Commonwealth** (REGIONAL_GOVERNMENT; Pluto) — Pluto civil government. Part of Pluto–Charon gateway; not Kuiper sovereign.
- **Mars System Coordinating Compact** (PUBLIC_COMPACT; Mars) — Interregional infrastructure, transfer coordination and common system policy. Coordinates without replacing strong regional Martian governments.
- **Mercury Process & Transit Board** (PUBLIC_NETWORK; Mercury) — Traffic control, hazardous-process licensing and life-safety coordination. Combined board reflects small population and extreme process-risk geography.
- **Inner Solar Process Standards Board** (PUBLIC_STANDARDS; Mercury/heliocentric) — High-temperature/process qualification and shared reference standards. Evidence/proof service rather than Mercury sovereignty.
- **Venus Navigation & Life-Safety Service** (PUBLIC_NETWORK; Venus) — Aerostat navigation, orbit-cloud transfer, habitat emergency coordination. Life-safety network grown from habitat operations rather than a generic transit ministry.
- **Venus Habitat Assurance Board** (PUBLIC_ASSURANCE; Venus) — Life-support, acid-environment materials and habitat assurance. Certification is habitat-safety-centered; not a general Solar-System standards monopoly.
- **Jovian Navigation & Rescue Compact** (PUBLIC_COMPACT; Jupiter system) — Radiation-aware navigation, rescue, convoy safety and gateway coordination. Member-jurisdiction compact; Callisto hosts much traffic but does not govern Ganymede/Europa/Io.
- **Jovian Metrology Forum** (STANDARDS_FORUM; Jupiter system) — Mutual recognition of measurement, quarantine and industrial reference standards. Forum/recognition network rather than a ministry with civil jurisdiction.
- **Saturn Intermoon Compact** (PUBLIC_COMPACT; Saturn system) — Route coordination, commerce arbitration and mutual rescue. Treaty structure among Titan and other local jurisdictions; Titan-led economy does not imply Titanian sovereignty.
- **Saturn Mutual Rescue & Security Treaty** (SECURITY_TREATY; Saturn system) — Mutual rescue, convoy protection and security coordination supplied by member jurisdictions. No standing Saturnian civil government or universal security directorate is implied.
- **Titan Calibration Bureau** (PUBLIC_TECHNICAL_BUREAU; Saturn system) — Widely recognized cryogenic/process calibration services. Technical service influence; not the sole Saturnian standards authority.
- **Uranus Free-Space Exchange Charter** (CHARTERED_NETWORK; Uranus system) — Traffic, interoperability, mutual-aid rescue and shared free-space infrastructure administration. Bundled functions reflect small system scale; moon civil jurisdictions remain autonomous.
- **Triton–Habitat Navigation Compact** (PUBLIC_COMPACT; Neptune system) — Navigation, transfer coordination and mutual rescue between Triton and free-space habitats. Triton is an anchor, not system sovereign.
- **Neptune Deep Standards Office** (TECHNICAL_OFFICE; Neptune system) — Compact metrology/qualification office embedded in Neptunian network institutions. Small office rather than a full independent standards directorate.
- **Kuiper Route & Rescue Compact** (PUBLIC_COMPACT; Kuiper network) — Route publication, navigation interoperability, rescue and mutual aid. Sparse network compact; Pluto–Charon remains gateway without becoming Kuiper government.
- **Elysium Orbital Works Consortium** (REGIONAL_COOPERATIVE; Mars) — Martian-owned heavy yard integration, utilitarian fleet construction and protected strategic ship-service capacity. Local challenger at Mars Orbital Shipyard Arc; Asteria remains major supplier/service ecosystem rather than universal yard owner.


## Frozen regional institutional morphology

The table below is a compact authority map, not a claim that every named body is a sovereign state. **Civil jurisdiction is local; network power propagates by function.** Repeated names in administrative/security columns reflect actual shared network functions, while regional institutional form varies by settlement history and scale.

| System | Political morphology | Network form | Civil authorities | Administrative/network authorities | Security/rescue authorities |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Earth | Plural terrestrial states/blocs; exact 2226 map open | No Solar-System-style functional stack; sovereignty remains territorial and plural. | African Union Federation; Australasian Compact; East Asian Compact; Eurasian Union; Latin American Community; North American Federation; South Asian Union | Atlantic Orbital Transport Authority; Australasian Orbital Authority; East African Orbital Authority; Equatorial Launch Cooperative; Eurasian Spaceflight Authority; Pacific Polar Spaceflight Authority; South Asian Orbital Authority; Southern Ocean Spaceflight Authority; Western Pacific Spaceflight Authority | African Union Federation; Australasian Compact; East Asian Compact; Eurasian Union; Latin American Community; North American Federation; South Asian Union |
| Earth Orbit | Chartered orbital commons + Earth-linked jurisdictions | Dense old infrastructure supports specialized traffic, liability, standards and Earth–Luna strategic bodies. | Earth Orbital Charter Assembly; Free Habitat League | Earth Metric Standards Directorate; Earth Orbital Traffic Compact; Solar Weather & Warning Authority | Earth–Luna Strategic Coordination Command |
| Moon | Polycentric lunar/cislunar jurisdictions | Lunar Transit & Standards Authority combines everyday transit/operating standards; metric certification remains a narrower cislunar function. | Cislunar Commonwealth Authority; Far-Side Science Trust; Lunar Orbital Charter Council; Lunar South Polar Commonwealth; Mare Industrial Authority | Cislunar Standards Directorate; Far-Side Science Trust; Lunar South Polar Commonwealth / mixed concession; Lunar Transit & Standards Authority; Mare Industrial Authority; Polar Volatiles Cooperative | Earth–Luna Strategic Coordination Command |
| Mars | Regional Martian polities + Mars System Coordinating Compact | System coordination integrates interregional transit and infrastructure; metric standards and defence/rescue remain separately accountable because scale and strategic maturity justify specialization. | Elysium Industrial Authority; Hellas Basin Union; Mars System Coordinating Compact; Northern Mars Commonwealth; Valles–Noctis Civic Compact | Elysium Industrial Authority; Hellas Basin Union; Mars Metric Standards Directorate; Mars Orbital Transit Authority; Mars System Transit Authority; Northern Mars Commonwealth; Northern Mars Commonwealth / concession operators; Valles–Noctis Civic Compact | Mars System Defence & Rescue |
| Mercury | Mercury Polar Commonwealth + orbital/process charters | Mercury Process & Transit Board combines traffic, hazardous-process licensing and life-safety; Inner Solar process standards provide external proof. | Mercury Orbital Charter Assembly; Mercury Polar Commonwealth | Inner Solar Standards Directorate; Mercury Polar Commonwealth; Mercury Transit Authority; Sunward Materials Consortium | Inner Solar Security Directorate |
| Venus | Aerostat/commonwealth and orbital habitat jurisdictions | Venus Navigation & Life-Safety Service combines atmospheric navigation, habitat emergency coordination and orbit-to-cloud transfer; standards are embedded in habitat assurance rather than a standalone ministry. | Venus Aerostat Commonwealth; Venus Orbital Charter Assembly; Venus Surface Science Charter | Venus Aerostat Commonwealth; Venus Atmospheric Industries; Venus Metric Standards Office; Venus Orbital Transit Authority; Venus Surface Science & Materials Authority | Venus Security & Disaster Response |
| Ceres | Ceres Commonwealth embedded in Belt network compacts | Belt transit, standards and rescue institutions are mature because Ceres is an old multi-body exchange; they do not constitute Belt sovereignty. | Ceres Commonwealth | Belt Standards Directorate; Belt Transit Authority; Ceres Commonwealth / concession operators; Ceres Volatiles Cooperative | Belt Security & Rescue Directorate |
| Belt Majors | Body-specific industrial/science polities and cooperatives | Bodies use local charters while mature Belt transit/standards/rescue compacts span the network. | Hygiea Resource Commonwealth; Pallas Scientific Council; Vesta Industrial Authority | Belt Transit Authority; Hygiea Resource Cooperative; Vesta Industrial Authority | Belt Security & Rescue Directorate |
| Jupiter | Independent moon jurisdictions + Jovian Navigation & Rescue Compact | Navigation, rescue and convoy safety are integrated because radiation geography makes them common survival infrastructure; metrology is coordinated through a forum rather than a sovereign standards ministry. | Callisto Commonwealth; Europa Ocean Protection Authority; Ganymede Civic Authority; Io Hazard Operations Charter; Jovian Free-Space Assembly | Callisto Commonwealth; Europa Ocean Protection Authority; Ganymede Civic Authority; Jovian Atmospherics Consortium; Jovian Metrology Forum; Jovian Navigation & Rescue Compact | Jovian Navigation & Rescue Compact |

## Canon boundaries

- Canonical names and broad roles are locked at this baseline.
- Exact megacorporate equity, subsidiary maps, merger histories and jurisdiction-by-jurisdiction market shares remain open.
- Artifact-era predecessor genealogies, exact Oumuamua sample allocations, Partition treaty organs, and precise corporate/state winner-loss histories remain open unless separately promoted.
- Continuity Naturalism, Expansionism and The Convergence are canonical movement families; exact organizations, leaders, membership and violent splinter names remain open.
- The Continuity Assembly, Lineage Cooperative Network and Autonomous Personhood League are the three largest interregional synthetic political traditions, not exhaustive factions.
- Security authorities are functional presence/jurisdiction constructs; detailed orders of battle remain open.
- Commercial leadership does not imply civil government, public certification or security sovereignty.

# SOURCE DIVISION — Extrasolar Atlas & Frontier Register

**Consolidated from:** `LOOM_2226_Extrasolar_Atlas_Frontier_Register_v1.1.md`

# LOOM 2226 — Extrasolar Atlas & Frontier Register v1.1

**Date:** 21 August 2026  
**Status:** GOVERNING PLAYER-FACING EXTRASOLAR CANON — XENOARCHAEOLOGY INTEGRATED  
**Scope:** Public/operational human knowledge at campaign start  
**Hidden topology:** explicitly excluded
**Xenoarchaeology:** 1I is Solar-access historical archaeology; Reef/Tau Ceti and Kite/Epsilon Eridani are verified independent lineages. Neither extrasolar site is canonically a homeworld.

> Ordinary distance remains real. Loom accessibility is relational. Geography is discovered, not painted onto a star map.

## 1. Historical spine

| Year | Event |
|---:|---|
| 2221 | Needle One completes first confirmed unmanned interstellar arrival and return at Alpha Centauri |
| 2222 | Probe rush; Alpha-side surveys reveal promising Tau Ceti and Epsilon Eridani relations |
| 2223 | Peregrine completes first crewed Sol→Alpha translation |
| 2224 | Persistent Alpha occupation; first human arrival at Tau Ceti; first crewed Barnard mission; anomalous mineralized structures become a major Tau survey target |
| 2225 | Tau Ceti settlement-intent cohort; **Reef technological lineage independently verified**; first crewed Sirius and Ross 128 missions |
| 2226 | First crewed Epsilon Eridani mission; **Kite technological lineage independently verified**; frontier remains tiny |

Approximate cumulative program state: 42 serious unmanned commits, 29 confirmed arrivals,
19 probe returns/recoveries, 6 unresolved non-returns, 58 crewed interstellar legs and zero crewed Loom fatalities.

## 2. Known human geography

| System | Distance from Sol | 2226 biological presence | Role |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| Alpha Centauri | ~4.37 ly | ~210 | primary bridgehead, route metrology, packet/logistics |
| Barnard's Star | ~5.96 ly | ~38 | research/metrology; commercially disappointing junction |
| Sirius | ~8.6 ly | ~22 | stellar/white-dwarf science |
| Epsilon Eridani | ~10.5 ly | ~11 | new survey/industrial-seed beachhead; active Kite xenoarchaeology emergency |
| Ross 128 | ~11.0 ly | ~6 | planetary/astrobiology survey |
| Tau Ceti | ~11.9 ly | ~64 | first settlement-intent community; Reef archaeological complex |

Total biological presence outside Sol: **~351**.

## 3. Public route picture

- Sol ⇄ Alpha Centauri: mature first-generation route.
- Sol → Barnard's Star: good outbound access; return engineering is less favorable.
- Alpha ⇄ Tau Ceti: mature/provisional operational route and preferred access.
- Alpha ⇄ Epsilon Eridani: newly crew-rated, unusually strong.
- Sol ⇄ Sirius: experimental/science route.
- Sol ⇄ Ross 128: experimental and conservatively operated.

Candidate observations exist beyond this list. Candidate does not mean traversed; traversed does not mean returned;
returned does not mean crew-rated.

## 4. Astronomy and settlement doctrine

### Alpha Centauri
Primary installation is free-space. No habitable Alpha A/B planet is required or canonized.

### Barnard's Star
Old M dwarf with a compact close-in small-planet system. Important science target; weak default surface-settlement case.

### Sirius
A1V primary plus white dwarf Sirius B. Exceptional stellar/compact-remnant laboratory; not a civilian colony.

### Tau Ceti
Nearby G-type star with low-mass planet candidates and debris environment. Strongest early settlement-intent case,
but the 2226 community remains engineered-habitat based unless later planetary dossiers earn a surface site.

Tau Ceti also contains the first extrasolar verified technological archaeology: a **Reef industrial/founder complex** confirmed in 2225. The site is not canonically the Reef homeworld. Its terminal strata show fixed living-industrial substrate systematically converted into compact founder/continuity systems. Terminal horizon is provisionally ~1.5–3 Ma.

### Epsilon Eridani
Young K dwarf with giant planet/debris architecture. Strong planetary-system science and automated-resource rationale;
not an assumed garden world.

The first crewed 2226 campaign is simultaneously confirming the **Kite technological lineage** through orbital membrane structures, compact departure craft and a broad terminal dispersal debris field. Epsilon Eridani is not canonically the Kite home system; it appears to be a terminal staging/dispersal complex. Terminal horizon is provisionally ~80–250 ka.

### Ross 128
Quiet M dwarf with temperate-regime super-Earth-mass Ross 128 b. High-value planetology/astrobiology target.
Atmosphere, biosphere and surface habitability remain open canon gates.

## 5. Economy

There is no meaningful bulk interstellar commodity market in 2226.
High-value flows are:
people, precision instruments, certified electronics, medical/biological stocks, fabrication systems,
samples, software, legal/financial state, intelligence and topology data.

The core route-data value chain is:

**observation → relational model → navigation solution → certified envelope**

The first boom is the **Loom Rush**. The first correction follows speculative overreach at Barnard and other weak candidates.

## 6. Packet civilization

No independent FTL communication exists.
Ships physically carry synchronized legal, market, scientific, cultural and personal state.
A trip may take hours while a message waits days for a carrier.

**Travel is fast. Authority is intermittent.**

## 7. Institutions

- ILNA — standards/mutual recognition.
- EMSD — Earth-orbital technical/certification secretariat.
- SSSC — scientific survey compact.
- Axiom — proof/metrology.
- Asteria — ship integration/yard support.
- ELSCC Loom Survey Cell — Earth–Luna strategic topology/security.
- Concord Mutual — assurance/underwriting.
- FarReach Survey — commercial route-prospecting entrant.
- Alpha Centauri Forward Station Charter Board — bridgehead local governance.
- Tau Ceti Settlement Cooperative — resident-heavy settlement governance.

## 8. Open gates

Not canonized here:
- indigenous extrasolar life;
- Earthlike open-air worlds;
- exact planetary resource grades;
- deeper public routes;
- causes of unresolved probe losses;
- exact classified route holdings;
- future Epsilon Indi access;
- the hidden 28-system physical graph.

## 9. GM separation

The fixed hidden topology is maintained in `LOOM_2226_Hidden_Loom_Graph_v0.1.md`.
It is model truth, not player-facing knowledge. Public canon must be updated only as routes are historically discovered.

# CORRECTED PROPULSION / WORLD INTEGRATION — GOVERNING v2.1

## Governing engineering lineage

The propulsion stack is governed in this order:

1. Conceptual Physics Engineering MVP v1.3 — foundational M1/M2 rules and hard kills.
2. Loom Engineering WP-1–WP-5 — first-generation Loom implementation.
3. Metric WP-8–WP-11 — first-generation sub-c Alcubierre implementation and certification.
4. Inherited Courier Integrated Propulsion Baseline v1.1 — current reference ship ledger.

The rejected phase-walk Metric Engineering MVP and the 0.03–0.05c constitutive lineage are historical only.

## Three-drive architecture

- **TORCH:** ordinary momentum propulsion.
- **METRIC:** continuous subluminal Alcubierre-family local spacetime engineering.
- **LOOM:** discontinuous nonlocal relational re-embedding.

Metric and Loom are mutually exclusive active states of the same unified relational plant.

## Correct reference courier

| Quantity | Governing value |
|---|---:|
| Wet mass | **~1,158.5 t** |
| Dry excl. 300 t working fluid/water | **~858.5 t** |
| Unified relational propulsion plant | **~88 t** |
| Active Mc-299m | **10.0 kg** |
| NRE active tiles | **100 × 100 g** |
| Distributed nodes | **208** |
| Shared reversible field bank | **2 GJ** |
| Cryogenic plant | **150 kW-class at 20 K** |
| NORMAL metric | **0.268c** |
| FAST metric | **0.437c** |
| EXPEDITE metric | **0.519c** |
| HARD metric | **0.595c** |
| Saturation response | **~0.678c, not certified** |
| FTL metric | **BLACK / hard kill** |

The old 45 t Loom + 19 t metric split, 1 GJ bank, ~1,134.5 t mass and phase-walk terminology are rejected regressions.

## Metric certification doctrine

The governing constitutive response is:

\[
\beta_{\rm machine}=\chi_M\frac{\mathcal J_M}{N_{\rm eff}}
\]

with \(\chi_M\) measured as a material/fixture property. Reference production target is 1.00;
accepted first-generation lots are 0.95–1.05 with ship-specific fit uncertainty \(\sigma_\chi\le0.01\).

Crew certification requires a smooth \(C^3\) wall, massive-particle geodesic shedding, and **no null caustic
or conjugate point intersecting the protected worldtube**. The real-time certifier uses Jacobi/ray-bundle
propagation; missed certification deadlines force HOLD → DERATE/REORIENT → COLLAPSE.

Background/Killing-energy accounting is preserved. The 2 GJ bank is field-control capital, not a gravity-energy
reservoir and not a free-work source.

Time-dependent sub-c operation passes the present screening, but a full 4D finite-RSET benchmark remains a
standing research/certification target rather than a solved fundamental theorem.

Clustered contiguous node losses are materially worse than equal-count random losses and govern degraded-operation doctrine.

## Metric operating geography — v2.4 supersession

Metric is not terminal maneuver propulsion. The earlier body-independent “ramp ×3 = physical clearance radius” rule is **superseded**. Clean-ramp displacement remains real, but it is metric path rather than ordinary torch stand-off.

Physical certification is derived from the local tensor/geodesic environment, distributed-node correction demand, coupled thermal/cryo load, null/matter/wake/RSET checks, valid calibration records and the ship/mode/ramp state. The resulting certification volume may be anisotropic and time varying.

Roadstead, traffic-control boundary, port and physical certification volume are separate. Atmospheres, dense rings, uncertain debris and unresolved bright-source/caustic geometries remain difficult or non-certified unless the actual ship/mode/environment solution passes.

For NORMAL, one clean linear ramp spans ~401,722 km of metric path and two ramps require ~803,444 km before any plateau. Earth–Moon and Mars–Phobos therefore cannot fit a full NORMAL acquire→plateau→collapse sequence from ramp geometry alone. No universal acquisition radius follows from that fact.

## World consequence

The corrected 0.27–0.60c metric envelope materially compresses long-baseline Solar travel compared with the
rejected 0.03–0.05c model. It does **not** erase ports, orbital mechanics, local torch legs or settlement geography,
because high metric operation requires finite ramp distance, environment-specific certification, traffic/roadstead access, surveyed clean geometry, thermal posture, healthy distributed nodes and conventional terminal-state work.

Solar civilization therefore becomes more tightly synchronized at the high-value network level while retaining
strong local geography and institutional chokepoints.

The existing extrasolar Loom frontier remains viable: Loom, not metric, is the FTL mechanism. Metric improves
staging, in-system survey, packet positioning and access to Loom-safe commit volumes; it does not replace the hidden
relational route graph.

No independent FTL communication exists.
